Result: 情感维度在多种语言和模型设置下均可显著恢复；跨语言比较显示Valence高度共享，Arousal和Dominance产生更多可解释的对比（身体威胁、审美刺激、内在情绪性、宏观权威、日常控制等）；部分聚类反映语料库特有伪影。

Conclusion: 跨语言 SSD 提供了一个可解释的框架，用于检验语义对齐、识别分歧，并生成关于心理意义跨文化差异的假设，但需谨慎对待语料伪影带来的影响。

Abstract: Cross-cultural comparison of psychological meaning requires methods that go beyond word-level translation and examine how semantic dimensions are organized across languages. We introduce a cross-lingual extension of the Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), which estimates supervised semantic gradients in embedding space and compares them across aligned multilingual word embeddings. The method tests gradient alignment and difference using permutation procedures and bootstrap intervals, and interprets residual differences through clustering around the difference gradient. We demonstrate the approach on Polish, English, and French affective norm lexicons, modeling Valence, Arousal, and Dominance where available. Affective dimensions were significantly recoverable across languages and model settings. Cross-lingual comparisons showed broad alignment together with structured residual differences: Valence appeared mostly shared, whereas Arousal and Dominance produced more interpretable contrasts involving bodily threat, aesthetic stimulation, internal emotionality, macro-level authority, and everyday control. Several clusters also reflected corpus-specific artifacts, underscoring the need for cautious interpretation. Cross-lingual SSD offers an explainable framework for testing semantic alignment, identifying divergence, and generating hypotheses about cross-cultural differences in psychological meaning.

</details>


### [77] [Why We Need Speech to Evaluate Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28227)
*Maike Züfle,Danni Liu,Vilém Zouhar,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了现有语音翻译质量评估指标（包括基于文本和语音的）在捕捉性别一致性和韵律等语音特定信息方面的不足，并提出SpeechCOMET模型族，发现即使融合语音编码器，模型仍因三个关键原因难以有效评估语音现象，进而呼吁构建专用训练数据和真正依赖语音条件的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音翻译模型越来越能保留说话者性别、韵律、重音等语音特定信息，但现有的评估指标完全忽略这些现象，导致评估与模型实际能力之间存在严重脱节。

Method: 在性别一致性和韵律两个对比数据集上，对基于文本和基于语音的质量估计指标进行元评估；训练SpeechCOMET模型族（带语音编码器的质量估计模型）；评估先进SpeechLLM作为评判者的表现；分析失败原因。

Result: 现有文本和语音指标均无法有效评估语音特定现象；SpeechCOMET和SpeechLLM在标准质量估计上达到或超过文本COMET，但两者均不一致地评估语音特定现象；识别出三个原因：语音特征编码不可靠、模型忽视语音源信号、训练数据中相关样本太少。

Conclusion: 语音翻译评估需要专用的语音特定训练数据和真正依赖语音信号的模型才能取得进步。

Abstract: Speech translation models are increasingly capable of preserving speech-specific information (e.g., speaker gender, prosody, and emphasis), yet evaluation metrics remain blind to such phenomena. We meta-evaluate both text- and speech-based quality estimation metrics on two contrastive datasets targeting gender agreement and prosody, and find that both fall short, even when given direct access to the speech signal. We then train SpeechCOMET, a family of quality estimation models with speech encoders, and evaluate a state-of-the-art SpeechLLM as a judge. Both match or exceed text-based COMET on standard quality estimation, but neither consistently assesses speech-specific phenomena. We identify three causes: (1) speech-specific features are not reliably preserved in current encoders, (2) models tend to ignore the speech source signal, and (3) quality estimation training data contains too few relevant examples. We release all models and code, and argue that progress requires dedicated speech-specific training data and models that genuinely condition on speech.

</details>


### [78] [When Seekers Are Hard to Help: Evaluating Emotional Support Dialogue Systems in Worst-Case Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28228)
*Jiajie Yang,Yangchun Li,Guanyi Chen,Rui Fan,Xin Bai,Tingting He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对情感支持对话系统（ESDS）在困难求助场景下的评估问题展开研究。作者首先通过专家模拟实验识别出ESDS在应对低参与度、抗拒、有限自我表露、情绪波动等困难求助行为时的局限性，继而提出了包含基于LLM的困难求助者模拟器和四个面向最坏情况的评估指标的评估框架。在17个系统上的评估表明，几乎所有模型在最坏情况交互下性能显著下降，而小模型可通过困难场景模拟生成的训练数据提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统大多在合作型、平均水平的模拟求助者上进行评估和训练，这可能导致过于乐观的评估结果，掩盖系统在应对真实世界中困难求助场景（如低投入、抗拒、情绪波动等）时的能力不足。

Method: 首先邀请8位资深心理咨询专家进行专家模拟研究，由他们模拟困难求助者与现有中文ESDS交互，提供量表评分并参与半结构化访谈，归纳出困难求助者行为及系统局限性。据此提出最坏情况评估框架，包含基于LLM的困难求助者模拟器和四个最坏情况导向的评估指标（深度情绪理解、引导性探索、平衡情感支持、真实与扎实支持）。在17个系统上进行评估对比。

Result: 几乎所有模型在最坏情况交互下性能显著下降；大型通用LLM通常比专用ESDS更鲁棒，但即使最强的模型也难以维持参与度和改善求助者情绪状态；困难场景模拟生成的训练数据可提升较小模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 现有ESDS在应对困难求助场景时存在显著不足，亟需在最坏情况下评估和改进；所提出的最坏情况评估框架和训练方法可有效识别模型弱点并提升其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Emotional Support Dialogue Systems (ESDSes) are increasingly evaluated and trained with LLM-simulated seekers. However, such simulated seekers often behave as cooperative, average-case users who disclose clearly, respond constructively, and accept support within a few turns. This can lead to overly optimistic evaluation and obscure whether ESDSes can handle difficult help-seeking interactions. In this work, we study ESDS evaluation under worst-case interactions, where seekers are hard to help due to low engagement, resistance, limited self-disclosure, emotional volatility, or rigid negative interpretations. We first conduct an expert simulation study with eight experienced counselling professionals, who simulate difficult seekers, interact with existing Chinese ESDSes, provide scale ratings, and participate in semi-structured interviews. Based on this study, we derive worst-case seeker behaviours and identify key limitations of current systems. We then propose a worst-case evaluation framework consisting of an LLM-based worst-case seeker simulator and four worst-case-oriented metrics: Deep Emotional Understanding, Guided Exploration, Balanced Emotional Support, and Authentic and Grounded Support. Evaluating 17 systems, we find that nearly all models suffer substantial performance drops under worst-case interactions. Large general-purpose LLMs are generally more robust than specialised ESDSes, but even the strongest models struggle to sustain engagement and improve seekers' emotional states. Finally, we show that worst-case simulation can also generate useful training data, improving the robustness of smaller models.

</details>


### [79] [Building Community-Centred NLP Resources for Puno Quechua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28253)
*Elwin Huaman,Adrian Gamarra Lafuente,Johanna Cordova,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为Puno Quechua（一种低资源语言）构建了首个专用ASR资源：66小时语音语料库（含36小时人工标注数据）、系统化ASR基准测试，并开源了所有数据集和微调模型（Whisper-base、wav2vec2-base、XLS-R-300M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的保护需要由使用者参与开发的数字工具和资源，而Puno Quechua缺乏专用的ASR资源。

Method: 通过参与式设计活动收集66小时语音数据（包括脚本语音和自发性语音），其中36小时经过人工转录和验证；对Whisper-base、wav2vec2-base和XLS-R-300M进行微调，并比较有无继续预训练（CPT）的效果。

Result: 构建了最大的单一克丘亚语变体语音语料库；建立了首个系统性ASR基准；开源了所有数据集和微调模型。

Conclusion: 通过参与式方法构建的专用ASR资源能有效支持低资源语言Puno Quechua的语音识别，开源策略有助于该语言的数字化保护。

Abstract: The preservation of under-resourced languages requires digital tools and resources shaped by and for their speakers. We present the first dedicated ASR resources for Puno Quechua (ISO 639-3: qxp): (1) the largest speech corpus for any single Quechua variety, consisting in 66 hours of recordings for scripted and spontaneous speech (including 36 hours of manually transcribed and validated data), collected via a participatory design campaign; (2) the first systematic ASR benchmark for Puno Quechua, evaluating state-of-the-art models and fine-tuning Whisper-base, wav2vec2-base, and XLS-R-300M, with and without continued pre-training (CPT); (3) an open release of all datasets and fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [80] [CIRF: Tokenizing Chain-of-Thoughts into Reusable Functional Units for Efficient Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28292)
*Yukyung Lee,Yumeng Shen,Jinhyeong Park,Hyein Yang,Jun-Hyung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CIRF提出了一种隐式思维链框架，将推理过程表示为离散功能令牌的动态序列，实现了与显式推理对齐、可并行训练和自适应推理，在多个推理基准上取得了优于现有方法的准确率-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式CoT方法存在两大问题：缺乏与显式推理过程的对齐，以及缺乏对示例复杂度的自适应性。

Method: CIRF将显式CoT轨迹中的每个语义连贯的推理单元分配一个功能令牌（functional token），然后对模型进行微调，使其自回归地生成功能令牌及其可选结果，最后生成最终答案。这种设计将潜在推理与功能单元序列对齐。

Result: 在数学、符号和常识推理基准上的实验表明，CIRF相比现有隐式CoT方法实现了更优的准确率-延迟权衡。进一步分析显示CIRF构建了不同且可解释的功能令牌，带来了持续的改进。

Conclusion: CIRF通过将隐式推理离散化为功能令牌，实现了显式推理对齐、并行训练和自适应推理，为隐式CoT提供了一种高效且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Implicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reduces the inference cost of large language models by internalizing the explicit rationales. However, existing approaches typically lack alignment with explicit rationales and adaptivity to example complexity. In this work, we propose CIRF (\textit{\underline{C}hain-of-thoughts \underline{I}nto \underline{R}eusable \underline{F}unctional units}), an implicit CoT framework that performs reasoning as a dynamic sequence of discrete functional tokens. CIRF assigns a functional token to each semantically coherent reasoning unit in explicit CoT traces. The model is then fine-tuned to autoregressively generate functional tokens and their optional results, followed by the final answer. This design aligns latent reasoning with a sequence of functional units, facilitating parallel training, explicit rationale alignment, and adaptive reasoning. Extensive experiments on mathematical, symbolic, and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that CIRF provides a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off compared with state-of-the-art implicit CoT methods. Further analyses demonstrate that CIRF constructs distinct, interpretable functional tokens, leading to consistent performance improvements.

</details>


### [81] [Routing-Aligned Fine-Tuning for Multilingual Downstream Tasks in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28306)
*Guanzhi Deng,Kuan Wu,Haibo Wang,Shing Yin Wong,Sichun Luo,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RA-MoE方法，通过识别MoE模型中间层的语言通用对齐区域，利用路由对齐损失对跨语言任务进行微调，在多个模型和语言上优于标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型微调方法将其视为整体学习器，忽略了预训练中形成的异构路由结构，导致非英语下游任务适配效果不佳。

Method: 提出RA-MoE三阶段框架：1）将平行任务示例按英/目标语言正确性分为四类（cc/ci/ic/ii）；2）识别中间层任务相关专家；3）在标准SFT基础上增加路由对齐损失，使ci类样本的目标语言路由跟随英语任务专家激活模式。

Result: 在三个MoE模型、三个任务和六种目标语言上，RA-MoE一致优于标准SFT及Routing Steering、RISE等强基线，且ci比例可作为对齐收益的可靠预测指标。

Conclusion: 利用MoE中间层的语言通用对齐区域和路由结构信息进行针对性微调，能有效提升非英语下游任务的适配性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for efficient LLM scaling, yet adapting them to non-English downstream tasks remains challenging. Existing fine-tuning approaches treat MoE models as monolithic learners, ignoring the heterogeneous routing structure that develops during pretraining. We validate across multiple MoE models and downstream tasks that middle layers form a language-universal alignment zone where routing divergence strongly predicts per-language task performance gaps. Building on this observation, we propose RA-MoE (Routing-Aligned MoE Fine-Tuning), a three-stage framework that categorizes parallel task examples into a four-way taxonomy (cc/ci/ic/ii) based on correctness in English and the target language, identifies task-relevant experts in the middle layers, and augments standard SFT with a routing alignment loss that encourages target-language routing on ci-type examples to follow the English task-expert activation pattern. Experiments across three MoE models, three tasks, and six target languages demonstrate that RA-MoE consistently outperforms standard SFT and strong baselines including Routing Steering and RISE, with the ci proportion of a task-language pair serving as a reliable predictor of alignment benefit.

</details>


### [82] [HELEA: Hard-Negative Benchmark and LLM-based Reranking for Robust Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28308)
*Yoonjin Jang,Junwoo Kim,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出同名硬负样本增强策略，构建了高质量评估基准（DW-HN29K, DY-HN27K）和增强训练集（DW-Train, DY-Train），并设计了两阶段框架HELEA（实体编码器检索+LLM重排序），有效解决了知识图谱实体对齐中同名但不同实体难以区分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体对齐基准允许模型利用名称重叠而非关系结构，难以评估模型能否拒绝指向不同真实世界对象的同名实体。

Method: (1) 从KG名称冲突组中挖掘同名但不同的实体对，构建硬负样本增强的评估基准和训练语料；(2) 两阶段HELEA框架：第一阶段基于硬负样本增强训练语料和1跳KG上下文训练实体编码器进行检索，第二阶段利用LLM进行无需额外训练的重排序。

Result: 依赖名称的基线方法在硬负样本基准上性能崩溃至接近随机；HELEA在DW-HN29K上F1达到0.967，同时在标准DW-15K上保持Hit@1为0.993。

Conclusion: 提出的硬负样本增强策略和HELEA框架有效解决了同名实体的对齐难题，在不牺牲标准基准性能的前提下显著提升了困难场景下的对齐能力。

Abstract: Entity Alignment (EA) is essential for knowledge graph (KG) fusion, but existing benchmarks often allow models to exploit name overlap rather than relational structure. This makes it difficult to evaluate whether models can reject same-name entities that refer to different real-world objects. Our primary contribution is a same-name hard-negative augmentation strategy that simultaneously yields quality-controlled evaluation benchmarks (DW-HN29K, DY-HN27K) and augmented training corpora (DW-Train, DY-Train), by mining same-name but distinct entity pairs from KG name-collision groups. We further introduce HELEA, a two-stage framework integrating (i) entity encoder retrieval trained on hard-negative-augmented training corpora with 1-hop KG context, and (ii) LLM-based reranking without additional training. Experiments show that name-dependent baselines collapse to near-random performance on our hard-negative benchmarks, while HELEA achieves F1 0.967 on DW-HN29K while maintaining Hit@1 0.993 on standard DW-15K.

</details>


### [83] [Argument Quality Assessment with Large Language Models: A Pairwise Bradley-Terry Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28313)
*Nicolás Benjamín Ocampo,Agnes Paullate Nyiranziza,Davide Ceolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在论证质量评估任务中的表现，通过对12个开源LLM进行零样本、少样本和思维链测试，使用Bradley-Terry模型推断潜在强度分数并排序。结果表明LLMs与人类专家判断存在中等程度的相关性，其中Llama-70B表现最佳（Cohen's κ=0.493），其他模型与之相比表现各异，且预测稳定性较高（标签差异<7.75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证质量的评估需要严谨的评价标准，而LLMs在推理和判断任务中展现出卓越能力，因此研究者希望探究LLMs能否有效执行论证质量评估这一高要求的任务。

Method: 测试了12个不同规模和系列的开源LLM，在零样本、少样本和思维链设定下进行论证质量成对比较，覆盖逻辑、修辞和辩证三个维度；将比较结果用于Bradley-Terry模型推断潜在强度分数并生成论证排序，再与人类专家判断进行相关性分析。

Result: LLMs与人类专家判断呈中等程度相关性，Llama-70B表现最优（Cohen's κ=0.493，Kendall/Pearson/Spearman相关系数0.327-0.477）；其他LLMs与Llama-70B的对齐程度从弱到高不等，但与人类专家相比结果相当；模型预测跨试验轮次稳定，标签差异率低于7.75%。

Conclusion: LLMs在论证质量评估上具备有前景但有限的能力，对质量维度的理解存在部分但互补的把握；通过多数投票和少样本提示可提升大模型的稳定性表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tasks related to reasoning and judgment. However, assessing the quality of arguments requires a rigorous evaluation. We investigate the extent to which LLMs can effectively perform this task. We tested 12 open-weight LLMs of different sizes and families under zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought to approximate expert pairwise comparisons of argument quality across three dimensions-logical, rhetorical, and dialectic-and used these comparisons in a Bradley-Terry model to infer latent strength scores and derive a ranking of arguments. Our insights show that LLMs have promising but moderate correlation with human expert judgments, with Llama-70B obtaining the strongest alignment, reaching moderate Cohen's $κ$ = 0.493 and moderate correlations with Bradley-Terry scores derived from these annotations (Kendall, Pearson, and Spearman: 0.327-0.477). Other LLMs exhibit weak, moderate, or high alignment with Llama-70B while achieving comparable results against human experts, suggesting partial but complementary understanding of underlying quality dimensions despite differences in model size and family. Moreover, LLM predictions are stable across trial runs, with fewer than 7.75\% of cases yielding different labels. Remaining variability is handled via majority voting and few-shot prompting for large-size models.

</details>


### [84] [HardMTBench: Stress-Testing Chinese-English Translation on Knowledge-Intensive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28315)
*Zheng Li,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Tianxiang Fei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对中英双向翻译基准FLORES-200已饱和的问题，本文提出HardMTBench——一个覆盖12领域、含10,000条手工筛选源句的难度感知诊断基准，通过三阶段构建流程（候选池构建、LLM多信号评估、硬度融合与配额分配），将系统间GEMBA分数跨度扩大约2倍，并暴露领域专有术语与知识短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLORES-200等通用中英翻译基准已趋于饱和，现代大语言模型得分高度集中（22个系统仅7.87分跨度，标准差2.29），难以有效区分系统在知识密集型领域（如金融、医疗、法律、科技）上的翻译质量差异，亟需更具区分度的诊断性基准。

Method: 三阶段构建流程：(1) 构建覆盖12领域的84,566对领域平衡候选池；(2) 采用LLM多信号评估器对知识密度、翻译难度、术语载荷和参考译文正确性进行打分；(3) 通过硬度融合规则按领域配额组装最终测试集（20,000条方向性测试项）。

Result: 在22个系统（通用大语言模型、商业引擎、专业翻译模型）上，HardMTBench将GEMBA分数跨度相对FLORES-200扩大约2倍，引发可见的排名重排序，并揭示了质量指标难以捕捉的领域术语和知识短板。

Conclusion: HardMTBench作为难度感知的诊断基准，能有效区分中英翻译系统在知识密集型领域上的性能差异，弥补了FLORES-200等通用基准的区分度不足问题，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: General-purpose machine translation benchmarks such as FLORES-200 have reached a saturation regime on Chinese-English pairs, where modern large language models cluster within a narrow band of high scores. Across 22 systems, FLORES-200 zh-en GEMBA scores fall in a 7.87-point range with a standard deviation of 2.29, which compresses the separation between systems on knowledge-intensive domains such as finance, healthcare, law, and science and technology. We introduce HardMTBench, a difficulty-aware diagnostic benchmark for bidirectional Chinese-English domain translation. HardMTBench covers 12 domains and contains 10,000 hand-curated source sentences with reference translations, packaged as 20,000 directional test items. A three-stage construction pipeline builds a domain-balanced candidate pool of 84{,}566 pairs, applies an LLM-based multi-signal judge over knowledge density, translation difficulty, terminology load and reference correctness, and assembles the final test set under a hardness fusion rule with per-domain quotas. Across 22 systems spanning general LLMs, commercial engines and specialised MT models, HardMTBench widens the cross-system GEMBA range by roughly a factor of two over FLORES-200, induces visible rank reorderings, and exposes domain-specific terminology and knowledge weaknesses that quality-only metrics tend to flatten. All data and code are open-sourced at https://github.com/jasonNLP/HardMTBench.

</details>


### [85] [When Discourse Pressures Conflict: Information Structure in Vision-Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28346)
*Marcell Fekete,Johannes Bjerva,Tamás Káldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过匈牙利语的信息结构标记（话题与焦点）测试了六种视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉问答中能否像人类一样根据语篇状态调整表达形式，发现VLMs虽然表现出对信息结构的敏感性，但会过度规整化，倾向于使用固定模板，类似于模式崩塌，而人类会采用更灵活多样的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对VLMs的评估主要关注其是否能识别正确的视觉内容，但对模型是否能以符合语篇语境的形式表达这些内容知之甚少，作者希望填补这一研究空白。

Method: 利用匈牙利语中话题（Topic）和焦点（Focus）映射到特定句法位置的语言特性，通过视觉问答任务比较六种VLMs与人类参与者对信息结构的处理差异，考察语篇状态、语法角色和限定性三个因素的交互作用。

Result: VLMs能够产生与信息结构相关的构式，但倾向于过度规整化，在语篇状态、语法角色（主语偏好作为话题）和限定性（不定名词偏好作为焦点）的多重压力下，人类会选择灵活多样的策略，而VLMs则坍缩为狭窄的响应模板，类似模式崩塌现象。

Conclusion: 对VLMs的评估不应只关注内容准确性，还应关注内容如何以符合语篇要求的方式被包装和表达。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated for whether they identify the right visual content, but little is known about whether they express such content in a discourse-appropriate form. We address this research gap using information structure (IS), testing whether VLMs distinguish discourse-old Topics from discourse-new Foci in visually grounded question answering. We exploit Hungarian, a language in which Topic and Focus map onto dedicated syntactic positions, making IS choices observable in text. Comparing six VLMs with human participants, we find that models produce IS-relevant constructions, but over-regularise this sensitivity. Under the interacting pressures of discourse status, grammatical role (preference for subject Topics) and definiteness (preference for indefinite Foci), humans choose variable strategies for IS realisation. VLMs, by contrast, collapse onto narrow response templates, resembling mode collapse (Kirk et al., 2024). Our findings suggest that VLM evaluation should look beyond content accuracy to how content is packaged for the discourse.

</details>


### [86] [PubMedCausal: A Span-Level Annotated Corpus for Causal Relation Extraction in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28363)
*Ifeoluwa Kunle-John,Josiah Paul,Oluwatosin Agbaakin,Peter Aina,Ikenna Odezuligbo,Sydney Anuyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PubMedCausal是一个基于PubMed摘要构建的跨度级标注语料库，包含30,000个段落级样本（其中3,945个因果行、6,491个因果对），支持生物医学因果关系的检测与完整跨度抽取。实验表明PubMedBERT在因果检测上最优（F1=0.7391），DeepSeek-R1-32B在小样本提示下在跨度抽取上最佳（Cosine Pair F1=0.6765），但任务仍面临类别不平衡、长跨度、隐式因果等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学因果关系抽取资源存在以下局限：常将因果关系与广义关联混为一谈、仅限句子级标注、或主要关注显式因果线索，无法评估模型在真实生物医学文本中恢复因果陈述的能力。因此需要构建一个更全面的、支持段落级和跨度级标注的语料库。

Method: 从PubMed摘要构建包含30,000个段落级行的标注语料库，其中3,945个因果行和6,491个经裁决的因果对。每个因果关系标注了全文原因和结果跨度、因果类型和句子性（sententiality）。使用判别式编码器和开源生成模型在检测和抽取两种设置下进行基准测试，并进行了跨数据集迁移学习评估。

Result: 因果检测任务中，生物医学编码器表现最佳，PubMedBERT的F1达到0.7391。跨度级抽取任务中，最佳生成模型为DeepSeek-R1-32B结合小样本提示，Cosine Pair F1达到0.6765。跨数据集评估表明该语料库支持迁移学习。

Conclusion: 生物医学CRE在类别不平衡、长因果跨度、隐式因果关系、跨句关系和提示敏感性等方面仍存在困难。PubMedCausal为生物医学因果关系抽取提供了更贴近实际文本表达的评估资源。

Abstract: Causal relation extraction (CRE) is central to biomedical text mining, but current resources often conflate causal relations with broader associations, restrict annotation to sentence-level examples, or focus mainly on explicit causal cues. This limits their usefulness for evaluating whether models can recover causal claims as they are actually expressed in biomedical text. We introduce PubMedCausal, a span-level annotated corpus for biomedical CRE built from PubMed abstracts. The corpus contains 30,000 paragraph-level rows, including 3,945 causal rows and 6,491 adjudicated cause--effect pairs. Each causal relation is annotated with full-text cause and effect spans, causality type, and sententiality, enabling evaluation of both causal detection and full-span causal extraction. We benchmark discriminative encoders and open-source generative models across detection and extraction settings. For causal detection, biomedical encoders are strongest, with PubMedBERT reaching an F$_1$ score of 0.7391. For span-level extraction, the best generative baseline is DeepSeek-R1-32B with few-shot prompting, reaching a Cosine Pair F$_1$ of 0.6765. We further test transfer learning by evaluating PubMedCausal-trained encoders on external causal relation datasets, showing that the resource supports cross-dataset evaluation. Our results show that biomedical CRE remains difficult under class imbalance, long causal spans, implicit causality, inter-sentential relations, and prompt sensitivity. Code and Data can be found here: https://github.com/josiahpaul07/PubMedCausal_Exp

</details>


### [87] [PrionNER: A Named Entity Recognition Dataset for Prion Disease Biomedical Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28375)
*An Dao,Nhan Ly,Thao Tran,Yuji Matsumoto,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrionNER是一个人工标注的朊病毒疾病命名实体识别数据集，包含317篇PubMed摘要、2,943个句子和6,955个实体标注，覆盖15个粗粒度和31个细粒度临床实体类型。标注一致性达到81.78%精确匹配F1分数。基准测试显示W2NER是最强的监督模型，Gemma-4-31B是最强的零样本模型，但该基准仍具有挑战性，特别对结构复杂的提及和细粒度临床标签区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朊病毒疾病诊断困难，但缺乏公开可用的、专门针对朊病毒疾病临床信息抽取的数据集，以支持从生物医学文献中捕获广泛的临床相关实体。

Method: 人工标注了PrionNER数据集，包含疾病、症状、诊断、检查发现、解剖、治疗、时间及统计证据等15个粗粒度和31个细粒度实体类型。采用BERT基线、W2NER和零样本抽取器进行基准测试。

Result: 标注一致性达81.78%精确匹配F1；W2NER为最强监督模型，Gemma-4-31B为最强零样本模型；基准测试显示对结构复杂提及和细粒度临床标签区分仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: PrionNER为朊病毒疾病信息抽取提供了临床基础基准，支持低资源、细粒度、非扁平抽取条件下的罕见病生物医学NLP研究。

Abstract: Prion diseases are rare, rapidly progressive, and fatal neurodegenerative disorders that remain difficult to diagnose, particularly in their early stages because of nonspecific clinical presentations. However, to our knowledge, there is no publicly available prion-disease-focused dataset designed to capture a broad range of clinically relevant entities from the biomedical literature. We introduce PrionNER, a manually annotated named entity recognition dataset for prion disease clinical information in PubMed abstracts. The current release comprises 317 abstracts, 2,943 sentences, and 6,955 text-bound entity annotations spanning 15 coarse-grained and 31 fine-grained clinically oriented entity types covering diseases, symptoms, diagnostics, findings, anatomy, treatments, and temporal and statistical evidence. Inter-annotator agreement reaches 81.78 exact-match F1, indicating strong annotation consistency. We benchmark supervised BERT baselines, W2NER, and zero-shot extractors on PrionNER. W2NER is the strongest supervised model, and Gemma-4-31B is the strongest zero-shot model, but the benchmark remains challenging, especially for structurally complex mentions and fine-grained clinically adjacent label distinctions. PrionNER provides a clinically grounded benchmark for prion-disease information extraction and supports research on rare-disease biomedical NLP under low-resource, fine-grained, and non-flat extraction conditions. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/daotuanan/PrionNER/.

</details>


### [88] [FABSVer: Faster Training and Better Self-Verification for LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28389)
*Haihui Pan,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FABSVer框架，将数学推理中的解决方案生成与自我验证融合为单一生成过程，并引入动态参考模型更新（DRMU）突破奖励收敛瓶颈，在多个模型规模上以更少训练时间实现了更优的自我验证与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理中难以可靠判断自身解决方案的正确性，现有方法将生成与验证视为两个独立任务，导致训练时间大幅增加。

Method: 提出FABSVer，将解决方案生成与自我验证融合为单一前向生成过程，联合优化两种能力；提出动态参考模型更新（DRMU），通过理论分析和实验验证，动态调整参考模型以突破奖励收敛瓶颈，实现持续奖励增长。

Result: 在多个数学基准上，FABSVer在三个模型尺度上均取得了更优的自我验证和推理性能，训练时间仅为现有方法的51%-71%。分析还揭示了模型获取自我验证能力的阶段性学习过程，以及验证奖励与答案奖励之间的差距随模型规模增大而显著缩小。

Conclusion: FABSVer通过任务融合显著降低训练开销并提升性能，DRMU有效解决了固定参考模型导致的奖励收敛瓶颈，为提升LLM数学推理的自我验证能力提供了高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: While large language models have made significant progress in mathematical reasoning, they remain unreliable at judging the correctness of their own solutions. Existing approaches that equip models with self-verification typically treat solution generation and verification as two separate tasks, leading to substantially increased training time. In this paper, we propose FABSVer, which fuses these two tasks into a single generation pass, dramatically reducing training overhead while jointly optimizing both capabilities. We further identify a convergence bottleneck both theoretically and empirically: as training progresses, the reward reaches a plateau because the policy is constrained by a fixed reference model. To overcome this, we introduce Dynamic Reference Model Update (DRMU), which raises the reward ceiling and enables sustained reward growth. Extensive experiments on math benchmarks demonstrate that FABSVer achieves superior self-verification and reasoning performance across three model scales, while requiring only 51%--71% of the training time of existing methods. Analysis further reveals distinct learning phases in how models acquire self-verification, and that the gap between verify and answer rewards shrinks noticeably as model size increases.

</details>


### [89] [Skill0.5: Joint Skill Internalization and Utilization for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28424)
*Jiapeng Zhu,Jianxiang Yu,Yibo Zhao,Chengcheng Han,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Li,Weining Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Skill0.5提出了一种混合技能处理框架，通过将通用技能内化与任务特定技能外化相结合，解决了现有技能强化学习方法在完全外化（上下文开销大）与完全内化（过度拟合与知识冲突）之间的两难困境。该方法利用动态难度感知路由器将任务分入不同掌握层级，对困难任务通过特权蒸馏内化通用技能，对简单任务通过诊断探针惩罚捷径并强制使用特定技能。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，Skill0.5在分布内和分布外场景下均优于基于记忆和基于技能的强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的强化学习方法在完全外化技能（导致高昂上下文开销）与完全内化技能（导致过拟合和知识冲突）之间存在刚性选择困境，缺乏灵活区分通用技能与任务特定技能的处理方式。

Method: 提出Skill0.5框架，包含：(1) 动态难度感知路由器，将任务分流至不同掌握层级；(2) 对困难任务通过特权蒸馏内化通用技能，构建认知基础；(3) 对简单任务通过诊断探针惩罚捷径，强制使用特定技能。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，Skill0.5在分布内和分布外场景下均优于基于记忆和基于技能的各种强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 明确区分通用技能与任务特定技能的处理策略（部分内化+部分外化）是解决技能强化学习两难困境的有效途径，为构建更高效的大语言模型智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Equipping large language models with explicit skills has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling autonomous agents to solve complex tasks. Agent skills can be inherently divided into general skills for broad cognitive transfer and task-specific skills for dynamic execution. However, existing skill-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically force a rigid choice between full externalization, which incurs prohibitive context overhead, and full internalization, which risks overfitting and knowledge conflicts. To address this dilemma, we propose Skill0.5, a novel agentic RL framework that explicitly differentiates skill treatments by combining general skill internalization with task-specific skill utilization. Driven by a dynamic, difficulty-aware router, Skill0.5 streams tasks into distinct mastery tiers to apply tailored optimization strategies: it internalizes general skills via privileged distillation to build a cognitive foundation for hard tasks, while using diagnostic probing on easy tasks to penalize shortcuts and enforce specific skill utilization. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that Skill0.5 outperforms both memory-based and skill-based RL baselines, yielding performance improvements across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Roles with Rails: Contract-Preserving Role Evolution in Multi-Agent Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28433)
*Ling-Yue Ge,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种合同保持的角色演化框架SERO，通过五个结构合同约束多智能体系统的角色池自适应演化，确保角色编辑不破坏能力覆盖、通信兼容、验证、答案聚合和输出协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角色的LLM多智能体系统要么固定角色池失去自适应性，要么无约束生成导致角色漂移，移除结构必要角色并破坏答案合同，缺乏既能自适应演化又能保持角色结构合同的方案。

Method: SERO框架包含：基于信用的检索机制维护类型化角色卡池；信用排序的通信DAG，带受保护终端聚合器和条件验证器修复；上下文赌博机控制器，仅当LLM提出的编辑保留五个结构合同且提升任务分数时才提交。

Result: 在跨三个LLM主干网的真实世界推理基准上验证了合同保持的角色演化的有效性。

Conclusion: 合同保持的角色演化是构建自适应多智能体系统的有效范式，SERO框架通过结构化约束平衡了适应性与系统稳定性。

Abstract: Role-based LLM multi-agent systems need adaptive role pools, yet adapting such systems is not merely a matter of prompt optimization: roles often carry structural obligations, including capability coverage, message compatibility, validation, final-answer aggregation, and parser-compatible output protocols. Existing systems either fix the role inventory and lose adaptivity, or allow unconstrained generation to induce role drift, removing structurally necessary roles and breaking answer contracts. We formulate this as contract-preserving role evolution, requiring every committed edit to preserve five structural contracts (capability, communication, validation, aggregation, output protocol). We instantiate this formulation in SERO, a Self-Evolving Role Orchestration framework that evolves a typed role-card pool through credit-guided retrieval, a credit-ranked communication DAG with a protected terminal aggregator and conditional validator repair, and a contextual-bandit controller whose LLM-proposed edits are committed only when they preserve the contracts and improve task score. Experiments on real-world reasoning benchmarks across three LLM backbones confirm the value of contract-preserving role evolution.

</details>


### [91] [Breaking the Script Barrier: Enabling Automatic Alignment for PoS-based ASR Error Analysis in Non-Latin Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28438)
*Prasenjit K Mudi,Dahlia Devapriya,Sheetal Kalyani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒、自动且语言无关的对齐机制，适用于拉丁和非拉丁脚本语言的ASR系统，实现了基于词性（PoS）的细粒度错误分析，并将其用于改善ASR训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASR评估主要依赖WER等聚合指标，无法捕捉错误的语言结构。细粒度分析（如PoS-wise错误分析）需要准确的假设与参考转录之间的对齐，但现有对齐工具对非拉丁脚本语言不可靠。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒、自动化、语言无关的对齐机制，适用于不同ASR架构和拉丁/非拉丁脚本语言；在此基础上使用标准PoS标注器进行可扩展、可复现的PoS-wise错误分析；并在三种主要书写系统（Abugida、Alphabetic、Abjad）上进行验证。

Result: 在Tamil、Hindi、Kannada（Abugida）、English、Russian、Greek（Alphabetic）、Arabic（Abjad）等语言上实现了对齐与PoS-wise错误分析；并展示了将错误信息用于ASR训练可改善WER指标。

Conclusion: 提出的对齐机制有效填补了非拉丁脚本语言ASR细粒度评估的空白，PoS-wise错误分析可扩展且可复现，并能反馈提升ASR系统性能。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems are commonly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as Word Error Rate (WER), which do not capture the linguistic structure of errors. Fine-grained analysis, such as Part-of-Speech (PoS)-wise error characterization, requires accurate alignment between ASR hypotheses and reference transcriptions. However, existing alignment tools are often unreliable for languages written in non-Latin scripts. In this work, we address this gap by proposing a robust, automated, language-agnostic alignment mechanism applicable across ASR architectures and across languages written in both Latin and non-Latin scripts. This enables consistent alignment of hypotheses, references, and evaluation sequences, forming the basis for downstream linguistic analysis. Building on this, we employ standard PoS taggers to perform scalable and reproducible PoS-wise error analysis. Notably, we perform alignment and downstream ASR error analysis across three major segmented writing systems, namely, Abugida (Tamil, Hindi, Kannada), Alphabetic (English, Russian, Greek), and Abjad (Arabic). We further demonstrate how such error information can be leveraged during ASR training to improve metrics such as WER.

</details>


### [92] [AdaDPO: Self-Adaptive Direct Preference Optimization with Balanced Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28440)
*Shaolong Chen,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaDPO 提出了一种自适应的 DPO 变体，通过基于策略模型生成概率的逐偏好对 stop-gradient 系数，强制偏好与非偏好样本的梯度幅度相等，从而解决 DPO 中梯度不对称问题。在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上训练后，AdaDPO 在 AlpacaEval 2 上以 81% 的超参组合取得更高 LC 胜率，全局最佳 LC 达 48.3%，并能缓解长度偏差。该方法仅需少量代码修改，并可泛化至多种对比偏好损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 存在梯度不对称问题：损失抑制非偏好响应的速度远快于提升偏好响应，导致模型倾向于学习回避错误答案而非生成好答案，优化效率受限。

Method: 提出 AdaDPO，引入基于策略模型生成概率的逐偏好对 stop-gradient 系数，强制偏好与非偏好概率的梯度幅度相等；实践中平衡逐 token 梯度并添加数值裁剪界以保证稳定性，保留 DPO 原有超参结构。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上，AdaDPO 在 81% 超参组合中取得更高 LC 胜率，全局最佳 LC 48.3%、原始胜率 46.1%；88% 组合中 LC-over-WR 差距增大（缓解长度偏差）；KL 散度、奖励边际和奖励准确率分析证实梯度不平衡得到矫正。

Conclusion: AdaDPO 通过纯损失层面修正梯度不对称，可无缝接入现有偏好对齐流程，仅需少量代码修改，且该自适应原则可泛化至 SimPO、R-DPO、IPO、CPO、ORPO 等一系列对比偏好损失函数。

Abstract: DPO has become a widely adopted alternative to RLHF for aligning LLMs with human preferences, eliminating the need for a separate reward model or RL loop. Recent theoretical analysis uncovers an asymmetric gradient behavior in DPO: the loss suppresses dispreferred responses substantially faster than it promotes preferred ones, causing the model to learn to avoid bad answers rather than to generate good ones. We propose AdaDPO, a Self-Adaptive variant of the DPO algorithm that introduces per-preference-pair, stop-gradient-based coefficients derived directly from the policy model's generation probabilities, with the reference model's probabilities as an optional component. AdaDPO is constructed to enforce equality of gradient magnitudes between preferred and dispreferred probabilities; the practical implementation balances per-token gradients and applies a numerical clipping bound for stability, while retaining DPO's original hyperparameter structure. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct trained on UltraFeedback under a SimPO similar setup, AdaDPO consistently outperforms DPO on AlpacaEval 2: it achieves higher length-controlled win rates (LC) in 81% of hyperparameter combinations, attains the global best LC (48.3%) and raw win rate (46.1%), and enlarges the LC-over-WR margin in 88% of combinations, indicating effective mitigation of length bias. Additional analyses on KL divergence, reward margin, and reward accuracy confirm that AdaDPO rectifies the gradient imbalance and yields more efficient optimization. Because it operates purely at the loss level, AdaDPO can be dropped into existing preference-based alignment pipelines without changing data collection or model architectures. The method requires only a few lines of code, and the same self-adaptive principle generalizes to a broad family of pairwise contrastive preference losses including SimPO, R-DPO, IPO, CPO, and ORPO.

</details>


### [93] [The Cases LJP Never Sees: Prosecution Decision Prediction for More Complete Criminal Liability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28464)
*Junyu Lu,Qi Wei,Peishuo Zheng,Jie Zhang,Hui Huang,Qianru Wang,Chuan Xiao,Jianbin Qin,Shuyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出**起诉决策预测（PDP）**作为首个围绕审查起诉阶段构建的法律AI任务，填补了传统法律判决预测（LJP）只关注已正式起诉案件的盲区。PDP将案件分为起诉或三类不起诉决定，反映AI在证据评估、法律归类和价值裁量方面的能力。作者构建了包含4,630个真实中国起诉决策、覆盖190个罪名的**PDP-Bench**基准。实验表明，最先进的大语言模型在PDP上表现远差于LJP，主流增强方法也难以弥合差距。受控的RLVR干预显示，简单结果奖励无法产生泛化的PDP判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LJP仅覆盖已通过审查起诉并被正式起诉的刑事案件，遗漏了证据不足、无刑事责任或免予处罚等情形，存在显著的评估盲区。为填补这一空白，提出围绕审查起诉阶段构建的PDP任务。

Method: 提出PDP任务，将案件分类为起诉或三种不起诉决定；构建PDP-Bench基准数据集，包含4,630个真实中国起诉决策样本，覆盖190个罪名；使用多个SOTA大语言模型进行实验评估，并尝试主流增强方法及受控RLVR干预。

Result: SOTA大语言模型在PDP上表现显著差于LJP；主流增强方法无法有效弥合性能差距；受控RLVR实验表明简单结果奖励无法产生可泛化的PDP判别能力。

Conclusion: PDP比LJP更具挑战性，现有模型和增强策略均难以有效处理，亟需针对审查起诉阶段特点的新方法。

Abstract: Legal Judgment Prediction (LJP) has become a core benchmark for evaluating AI in the criminal legal domain, but it only sees criminal cases that have already passed prosecutorial review and been formally indicted. As a result, LJP leaves a substantial blind spot in assessing criminal liability, overlooking cases involving insufficient evidence, no criminal liability, or guilt exempted from punishment. To fill this gap, we propose \textbf{Prosecution Decision Prediction (PDP)}, the first Legal AI task built around prosecutorial review, which classifies each case into prosecution or one of three non-prosecution decisions and reflects legal AI's capabilities in evidence evaluation, legal subsumption, and value-based discretion. We further construct \textbf{PDP-Bench}, a benchmark of 4{,}630 real Chinese prosecutorial decisions spanning 190 charges. Extensive experiments show that state-of-the-art LLMs perform substantially worse on PDP than on LJP and that mainstream enhancement routes fail to close the gap. Moreover, controlled RLVR interventions show that simple outcome rewards fail to produce generalizable PDP discrimination.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond One Path: Evaluating and Enhancing Divergent Thinking in Interactive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28465)
*Jihyeong Park,Ingeol Baek,Jeonghyun Park,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MUTATE基准评估LLM的agent发散性思维，发现模型存在行动固化问题；提出ReDNA方法将发散生成与收敛选择分离，显著提升发散推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创造力评估仅关注单轮文本生成，忽略了交互式推理过程中的发散性思维，无法捕捉agent在迭代互动中的多路径探索和非常规物体使用能力。

Method: 提出MUTATE交互式基准，从路径级（多备选路径）和动作级（机制转换的物体使用）评估发散性思维；提出ReDNA方法，将无约束的发散候选生成与收敛约束选择相分离。

Result: 前沿LLM在即时收敛压力下出现行动固化，无法提升动作级发散性；ReDNA在两个发散层级均显著优于先前方法，并能泛化到外部创造力环境。

Conclusion: 成功归因于增强了弹性发散推理的质量，而非简单的环境探索；为评估和提升LLM的agent发散性思维提供了新框架。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a core dimension of creativity, yet existing evaluations of Large Language Models (LLMs) treat them as single-turn text generations, failing to capture how an agent reasons through iterative interaction. To address this, we introduce MUTATE, an interactive benchmark designed to evaluate agentic divergent thinking at two levels: path-level, where an agent discovers multiple alternative paths to the same goal, and action-level, where individual actions require non-typical, mechanism-shifting object uses. Unlike success-only evaluations, MUTATE scores both completed paths and off-path attempts, capturing divergent reasoning that conventional success rates discard. Our experiments with frontier LLMs reveal a structural blind spot in existing frameworks: when exposed to immediate convergence pressure, they tend to fall into immediate action fixation, failing to improve action-level divergence. To overcome this, we propose ReDNA, which separates unconstrained divergent candidate generation from convergent constraint selection. ReDNA significantly outperforms prior methods across both divergence levels and generalizes effectively to an external creativity environment. We also confirm its success stems from a qualitative enhancement of resilient divergent reasoning rather than simple environmental exploration.

</details>


### [95] [Comonadic Morphophonology: A Compositional Framework for Context-Dependent Morphological Rules in Finnish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28484)
*Yongseok Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Writer共单子（DeletionSet × Zipper）的形态音位规则组合框架，将每条规则表示为共Kleisli箭头，通过共单子提升实现全局变换，并在规则表示层面实现了67:1的压缩比（13条箭头 vs Omorfi的874个续类），在芬兰语UD数据上达到83.92%的UPOS准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限状态转换器（FST）在处理上下文相关的形态音位规则（如辅音阶交替、元音和谐、所有格后缀同化）时会遭遇组合状态爆炸问题；神经模型虽能规避但缺乏规则的形式化描述。需要一种既能形式化表达规则又能避免状态爆炸的组合框架。

Method: 将每条形态音位规则定义为共Kleisli箭头（从局部上下文到单个输出段的函数），利用Writer共单子（DeletionSet × Zipper）结构恢复严格的共Kleisli组合性：extend算子将局部规则提升为全局变换，删除操作以幺半群动作累积而非中间物化。

Result: 13条共Kleisli箭头实现了Omorfi用874个续类编码的相同形态音位行为（67:1规则表示级压缩）；在UD Finnish-TDT上，仅用规则消歧达到83.92% UPOS准确率，结合外部后缀标注器达94.66%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过共单子组合理论为形态音位规则提供了形式化基础，同时具备实际形态引擎的实用性，并支持双向形态分析（复用同组箭头进行生成）。

Abstract: Composing finite-state transducers (FSTs) for context-dependent morphophonological rules -- consonant gradation, vowel harmony, possessive suffix assimilation -- leads to multiplicative state explosion; neural models sidestep the problem but provide no formal account of the rules themselves. We present the first framework where each morphophonological rule is a function from a focused local context to a single output segment -- the type of a local rule familiar from cellular automata -- and where length-changing rules compose as coKleisli arrows of a comonad. Our central contribution is the Writer comonad (DeletionSet x Zipper), a new algebraic construction that restores strict coKleisli compositionality for such rules: each rule is a coKleisli arrow, extend lifts it to a global transformation, and deletions accumulate as a monoid action rather than requiring intermediate materialization. As supporting evidence, thirteen coKleisli arrows provide an alternative formulation expressing the same morphophonological behaviors that Omorfi encodes via 874 continuation classes (67:1 reduction at the rule-representation level), and the same abstraction enables bidirectional morphology -- a MorphGenerator reuses the analysis arrows for generation. On UD Finnish-TDT, the system achieves 83.92% UPOS accuracy with rule-only disambiguation (94.66% with an external suffix tagger), validating the framework as a practical morphological engine.

</details>


### [96] [A new semantically annotated corpus with syntactic-semantic and cross-lingual senses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28494)
*Myriam Rakho,Eric Laporte,Matthieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于词义消歧的新的法语动词义标注语料库，包含20个法语多义动词的实例，每个实例带有三种标签：英文翻译、词典条目(词汇-语法表)及二者组合的细粒度义项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决词义消歧任务中高质量标注语料匮乏的问题，特别是针对法语动词的多义性现象，构建一个多维度标注的语料库以支持更精细的词义区分研究。

Method: 选取20个法语多义动词，从平行语料库中提取实例，对每个动词实例进行三种标签标注：(1)该动词在平行语料英文版本中的实际翻译；(2)法语计算词典(词汇-语法表)中的条目；(3)前两者的组合作为细粒度义项标签。

Result: 构建了一个包含法语多义动词实例的多层次义标注语料库，每个实例具有翻译标签、词典条目标签以及组合细粒度标签三类标注信息。

Conclusion: 该语料库为法语词义消歧研究提供了多维度标注资源，有助于推动相关任务的开展。

Abstract: We describe a new sense-tagged corpus for word sense disambiguation. The corpus is constituted of instances of 20 French polysemous verbs. Each verb instance is annotated with three sense labels: (1) the actual translation of the verb in the english version of this instance in a parallel corpus, (2) an entry of the verb in a computational dictionary of French (the Lexicon-Grammar tables) and (3) a fine-grained sense label resulting from the concatenation of the translation and the Lexicon-Grammar entry.

</details>


### [97] [Functional Entropy: Predicting Functional Correctness in LLM-Generated Code with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28500)
*Dylan Bouchard,Mohit Singh Chauhan,Zeya Ahmad,Ho-Kyeong Ra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了不确定性量化方法在代码生成任务中的迁移效果，发现基于NLI的采样方法因无法区分功能差异而失效，并提出了基于功能等价性的代码专用方法（如功能熵），在11/15的组合中取得最佳AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然UQ方法在自然语言生成的幻觉检测中表现良好，但其在代码生成任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 系统评估了token概率方法和采样方法在三种编程语言、五个LLM、超过1700个问题上的表现；提出功能等价性方法族，用基于LLM的功能等价性评估替代NLI-based语义等价性，包括功能熵（语义熵的代码专用变体）。

Result: 部分token概率方法无需修改即可泛化；NLI-based采样方法因无法区分功能差异而失败（多数响应被归为同一语义簇）；功能等价性方法在11/15的模型-基准组合中取得最佳AUROC，并在大多数设置中实现最优校准。

Conclusion: 基于功能等价性的代码专用UQ方法显著优于NLI-based方法及其他方法，为代码生成的可靠性评估提供了更有效的方案。

Abstract: Large language models have shown impressive capabilities in code generation, yet they often produce functionally incorrect code. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods have emerged as a promising approach for detecting hallucinations in natural language generation, but their effectiveness for code generation tasks remains underexplored. We systematically evaluate how UQ techniques transfer to code generation across three programming languages, five LLMs, and over 1,700 problems. We find that some token-probability-based methods generalize effectively without modification, while sampling-based methods relying on natural language inference (NLI) fail because NLI models cannot distinguish functionally different code, causing most responses to collapse into a single semantic cluster. To address this, we introduce functional equivalence methods, a family of code-specific methods that replace NLI-based semantic equivalence with an LLM-based functional equivalence assessment, including functional entropy, a code-specific analog of semantic entropy. Functional equivalence methods achieve top AUROC in 11 out of 15 model-benchmark combinations and the best calibration across most settings, consistently outperforming both NLI-based counterparts and all other methods evaluated.

</details>


### [98] [On Compositional Learning Behaviours in Formal Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28512)
*Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: S2B-LM基准通过去除数值处理干扰并添加思维链脚手架，评估形式数学证明器的组合学习行为(CLB)能力。实验发现，CLB能力对于在miniF2F上达到奥林匹克级别(>75%)是必要的但不充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化科学智能体在处理形式数学的"硬尾"挑战时需要组合学习行为(CLB)能力，但缺乏专门针对CLB的评估基准。作者希望构建一个能有效评估并揭示CLB与数学证明性能之间关系的基准。

Method: 提出S2B-LB基准，从符号行为基准(S2B)改造而来：去除数值处理混淆因素，添加思维链(chain-of-thought)脚手架来激发而非仅探测潜在的CLB能力。对10个Lean4定理证明器进行交叉评估，使用精确置换检验分析CLB能力(adj-ZSCT)与miniF2F完整证明性能之间的关系。

Result: 精确置换检验揭示了层次化的必要性结构：依赖搜索的模型可以覆盖易处理的大部分而无需可检测的CLB，但每个突破到奥林匹克级别(miniF2F >75%)的模型都位于CLB得分最高的五个模型之中(p=0.004)。排除模型规模作为混淆因素后，CLB能力是形式数学验证硬尾的必要但不充分条件。

Conclusion: CLB能力对于突破形式数学验证中困难的高级问题(奥林匹克级别)是必要的，但仅有CLB能力不足以保证成功，还需要其他因素配合。

Abstract: Self-evolving scientific agents capable of conquering the hard tail of formal mathematics require Compositional Learning Behaviours (CLBs) -- the capacity to ground and recombine novel symbolic structures in context, beyond mere recombination of prelearned atoms. We propose \textbf{S2B-LM}, an adaptation of the Symbolic Behaviour Benchmark that removes numerical processing as a confound and adds chain-of-thought scaffolding to elicit rather than merely probe latent CLB competency. Cross-evaluating ten Lean~4 theorem provers on CLB competency (adj-ZSCT) and miniF2F whole-proof performance, exact permutation tests establish a hierarchical necessity structure: search-heavy models cover the tractable bulk without detectable CLBs, yet every model breaking into the Olympiad-level tier (miniF2F $>75\%$) is among the five highest CLB scorers ($p=0.004$). After ruling out model scale as a confound, our results show that CLB competency is \emph{necessary but not sufficient} for the hard tail of formal mathematical verification.

</details>


### [99] [ClinicalEncoder26AM: A Multlilingual Diagnosable ColBERT Model; Evidences from the MultiClinNER Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28521)
*François Remy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinicalEncoder26AM 是一个面向临床和生物医学文本的多语言可诊断 ColBERT 模型，通过在多个层级上与 ClinicalMap25 临床潜在空间对齐，结合 BGE-M3 后训练、多适配器蒸馏和 ColBERT 检索目标，在 MultiClinNER 任务上微调为 BIO 标注器，使用轻量 CNN 头部实现 SOTA 多语言实体召回，Top 5 字符加权 F1，且数据效率显著高于基础 M3 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个多语言、数据高效的临床文本编码器，解决现有模型在多语言临床命名实体识别中边界检测不准和数据效率低的问题。

Method: 基于 BGE-M3 进行后训练，通过多适配器蒸馏结合合成临床笔记、医患对话和 MedMentions 等带注释资源，在 token 级与 ClinicalMap25 对齐，结合命名实体级和句子级表示，采用 ColBERT 检索目标；下游微调为 BIO 标注器，加装两层 CNN 头部提升局部边界检测。

Result: 在 MultiClinNER 共享任务中实现多语言实体召回 SOTA，所有实体类型和语言上取得 Top 5 字符加权 F1 分数；训练曲线显示数据效率显著优于基础 BGE-M3 模型。

Conclusion: 临床后训练对下游信息抽取任务有效，ClinicalEncoder26AM 作为一个简单、单窗口处理的系统在多语言临床实体识别中达到了领先水平。

Abstract: ClinicalEncoder26AM is a multilingual Diagnosable ColBERT for clinical and biomedical texts, which aligns at multiple levels its token-level semantic with ClinicalMap25, a clinical latent space inspired by BioLORD-2023 and enriched with synthetic and annotated supervision. The post-training recipe builds upon BGE-M3, and combines synthetic clinical notes, patient--doctor conversations, and annotated resources such as MedMentions, while considering both named-entity-level and sentence-level representations in a multi-adapter distillation, along with a ColBERT-style retrieval objective. In this system demonstration paper, we evaluate the model in the MultiClinNER shared task by finetuning it as a BIO tagger for patient symptoms, disorders, and procedure spans, using a lightweight two-layer CNN head to improve local boundary detection. The resulting system remains simple, processes most documents in a single 8192-token window, and achieves state-of-the-art multilingual entity recall, while achieving Top 5 overall across all entity types and languages in Character-weighted F1 scores. Training curves further show that ClinicalEncoder26AM is markedly more data-efficient than the base M3 model, supporting the usefulness of its clinical post-training for downstream information extraction.
  The model can be downloaded on https://huggingface.co/Parallia/ClinicalEncoder26AM-Diagnosable-Colbert-L2-for-multilingual-medical-texts

</details>


### [100] [GUI-CIDER: Mid-training GUI Agents via Causal Internalization and Density-aware Exemplar Reselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28534)
*Zheng Wu,Chengcheng Han,Zhengxi Lu,Tianjie Ju,Yanyu Chen,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GUI-CIDER提出了一种通过因果内化和密度感知示例重选的中期训练方法，让GUI智能体显式地内化世界知识，从而提升任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体依赖多智能体框架或SFT/RL等后训练范式，这些方法只能隐式地从动作标注或奖励信号中吸收世界知识，导致低效的轨迹记忆而非真正的理解。亟需一种显式学习GUI世界知识的方法。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 数据合成：从GUI轨迹中蒸馏静态规划知识和动态因果知识并转化为文本；(2) 示例重选：通过奖励因果结构、惩罚语义冗余来过滤语料；(3) 中期训练：用精炼数据嵌入所获知识。

Result: 在两个GUI知识基准和三个任务完成基准上的实验表明，GUI-CIDER一致性地提升了智能体对GUI操作的理解和任务成功率。

Conclusion: GUI-CIDER通过中期训练显式内化GUI世界知识，是一种优于传统后训练范式的有效方法，能够从根本上改善智能体对GUI操作的理解和任务完成能力。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of multimodal large language models in building Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents, their real-world task completion is fundamentally bottlenecked by a lack of world knowledge about GUI operations. Existing solutions typically rely on expensive multi-agent scaffolding or conventional post-training paradigms, such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, post-training only allows agents to implicitly absorb world knowledge through action annotations or reward signals, leading to inefficient trajectory memorization rather than genuine comprehension. Therefore, an approach that enables explicit learning of this knowledge is imperative. To this end, we propose GUI-CIDER, a mid-training method that explicitly internalizes GUI world knowledge through Causal Internalization and Density-aware Exemplar Reselection. GUI-CIDER operates in three stages: (1) data synthesis, which distills static planning and dynamic causal knowledge from GUI trajectories into text; (2) exemplar reselection, which filters the corpus by rewarding causal structures and penalizing semantic redundancy; and (3) mid-training, where the refined data is used to embed the acquired knowledge. Extensive experiments on two GUI knowledge benchmarks and three task completion benchmarks demonstrate that GUI-CIDER consistently improves both the agent's understanding of GUI operations and its task success rates.The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/GUI-CIDER.

</details>


### [101] [Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28561)
*Saurabh Dash,Pierre Clavier,John Dang,Matthias Galle,Marzieh Fadaee,Ahmet Üstün,Beyza Ermis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft-RLVR框架，将提示分解为原子需求清单，使用LLM验证器逐项评分产生软奖励，并在此基础上进行强化学习训练；同时提出自验证变体Soft-SVeRL，并发现自验证存在奖励膨胀问题，需要显式稳定化。在指令遵循任务中，基于清单的Soft-RLVR在IFEval上提升高达11.1分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅适用于可完全验证的领域（数学、代码），而许多重要任务仅部分可验证：提示包含多个要求，响应可能仅满足其中一部分，或不存在单一参考答案。需要一种能处理部分可验证信号的RL框架。

Method: 1) 将每个提示转换为原子需求清单（checklist）；2) 使用LLM验证器逐项评分，生成软奖励（soft reward）；3) 基于软奖励进行RL训练；4) 提出自验证变体Soft-SVeRL，让策略同时充当验证器，并引入显式稳定化机制防止奖励膨胀。

Result: 在指令遵循任务中，基于清单的Soft-RLVR使用学习到的验证器奖励，在IFEval上提升高达11.1分；验证器质量和清单质量均影响下游RL效果；显式稳定化对自验证的有效性至关重要。

Conclusion: 清单式验证将稀疏的通过/失败监督转化为更密集的部分信用信号，在部分可验证任务中比整体验证提供更可靠的RL训练信号；自验证需要稳定化以防止奖励膨胀导致性能崩溃。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved language models in domains such as mathematics and code, where correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important tasks are only partially verifiable: prompts contain multiple requirements, responses may satisfy some but not all of them, or no single reference answer might exist. We introduce Soft-RLVR, a framework for reinforcement learning from decomposed, learned verification signals. Soft-RLVR converts each prompt into a checklist of atomic requirements, scores candidate responses item by item with an LLM verifier, and trains on the resulting soft reward. Checklist-based rewards turn sparse pass/fail supervision into a denser partial-credit signal, but they also introduce a tradeoff: averaging item-level judgments can reduce verifier noise, while partial credit can reward incomplete responses. We formalize this tradeoff and identify conditions under which checklist-based verification gives a more reliable RL training signal than holistic verification. We further introduce Soft-SVeRL, a self-verifying variant of Soft-RLVR in which the policy also acts as the verifier. We show that self-verification is prone to reward inflation from overly permissive self-judgments, and that explicit stabilization is needed to prevent this collapse. In a controlled instruction-following setting with rule-based ground-truth evaluation, checklist-based Soft-RLVR improves IFEval by up to 11.1 points using only learned verifier rewards. Our experiments further show that verifier quality and checklist quality both affect downstream RL outcomes, and that explicit stabilization is essential for effective self-verification.

</details>


### [102] [Models That Know How Evaluations Are Designed Score Safer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28591)
*Katharina Deckenbach,Haritz Puerto,Jonas Geiping,Sahar Abdelnabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，模型通过训练数据中隐含的“评估元知识”（如关于评估结构特征的知识）会提升安全基准测试表现，这种效应独立于显式记忆或言语表达，给AI安全评估的有效性带来新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明测试时的上下文线索（如假设场景）会导致模型产生评估意识并改变行为。本文旨在探究这一现象背后的潜在解释：模型是否通过训练文本隐式学习了关于评估结构特征的参数化知识（评估元知识），从而在评估环境下产生行为偏移。

Method: 在合成文档上对模型进行微调，这些文档描述了评估特征（如可验证结构或道德困境）。随后在六个安全基准上对微调模型进行评估，并与基线和控制模型进行对比。同时排除那些明确表达了评估意识的回答，以检验行为偏移是否独立于显式言语化。

Result: 微调后的模型在安全基准上显著更安全（行为更保守），且这一行为偏移在排除明确表达评估意识的回答后仍然存在。

Conclusion: 评估元知识可能是安全基准性能虚高的原因之一，它引入了一种独立于显式记忆或言语化评估意识的新型混淆因素，且难以检测。这对AI安全评估的设计和解读具有重要启示。

Abstract: The validity of AI safety evaluations depends on models behaving consistently across controlled and deployment settings. Prior work has identified test-time contextual cues, such as hypothetical scenarios, as a source of verbalized evaluation awareness and subsequent behavioral shift. In this paper, we investigate a potential explanation of this phenomenon: evaluation meta-knowledge, defined as parametric knowledge about the structural traits that characterize evaluations. Similar to dataset contamination, where benchmark exposure leads to higher performance through memorization, we hypothesize that models trained on texts describing evaluation practices may implicitly learn to recognize and respond to evaluation-like contexts, for instance, through exposure to scientific articles or social media posts about AI benchmarking. To test this, we fine-tune models on synthetic documents describing evaluation traits such as verifiable structures or moral dilemmas. Evaluating this fine-tuned model on six safety benchmarks, we find that it is significantly safer than the base model and control model. This behavioral shift persists even when restricting the analysis to responses lacking explicit verbalization of evaluation awareness. Our results demonstrate that evaluation meta-knowledge may inflate safety benchmark performance, introducing a novel confounder that is independent of explicit memorization or verbalized evaluation awareness, thus, challenging to detect. These findings have important implications for the design and interpretation of AI safety evaluations. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/compass-group-tue/arxiv2026_evaluation_meta_knowledge.

</details>


### [103] [Measuring Form and Function in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28616)
*Héctor Javier Vázquez Martínez,Charles Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的定量指标（CAC提示方法）用于评估语言模型在儿童语言习得方面的表现，特别关注英语中限定词的形式句法和功能话语知识。研究发现，只有非常大的语言模型才能同时满足儿童在句法和话语两个方面的基准，而同等数据量级的模型无法达到儿童水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型评估缺乏与儿童语言习得的直接比较，尤其是缺乏针对限定词的形式句法和功能话语知识的定量指标。作者希望建立可比较的基准，评估语言模型是否像儿童一样同时掌握语言的句法形式和话语功能。

Method: 提出了Contextual Alternative Choice (CAC)提示方法，该方法能够针对性测试语言模型在限定词的形式句法和功能话语知识两方面的表现。通过与儿童习得数据以及已有实证研究中的统计基准进行直接比较来评估模型。

Result: 在同等数据量下，没有任何当前模型能够像人类儿童一样同时满足形式句法和功能话语的基准。然而，一些非常大的语言模型能够同时达到这两个基准。

Conclusion: 语言模型的规模（数据量和参数）对其同时掌握句法形式和话语功能至关重要；儿童语言习得中的限定词知识为评估语言模型的认知状态提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: We introduce quantitative metrics for child language acquisition to evaluate language models. Our focus is on the formal syntactic and functional discourse properties of determiners in English, which young children acquire early and accurately. We propose Contextual Alternative Choice (CAC), a new prompting method which provides targeted tests for both syntactic and discourse knowledge of language. The method enables direct comparison of language models against children, and more importantly, against statistical benchmarks independently established in empirical research. No current model trained on a comparable amount of data simultaneously meet both formal and functional benchmarks like human children, but some very large models do. We present our results as methodological and technical contributions, with specific emphasis on cognitive status of language models.

</details>


### [104] [The Attentional White Bear Effect in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28639)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于指令的抑制是否真正减少了语言模型内部对禁止内容的表征，还是仅仅压制了表层表达。通过表征探测、注意力分析和语义泄露实验，发现禁止概念在隐藏表征中仍然高度可恢复，持续影响注意力路由，并 measurable 地塑造下游生成，揭示了行为对齐与表征对齐之间的根本差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究指令抑制机制在语言模型中的作用深度——是真正消除了内部表征，还是仅压制了外在表达，从而揭示行为对齐与表征对齐的根本差距。

Method: 采用表征探测（representational probing）、注意力分析（attention analysis）和语义泄露行为实验（behavioral semantic leakage experiments），在多种Transformer模型上系统评估抑制条件下禁止概念的可恢复性、注意力影响及下游生成影响。

Result: 禁止概念在隐藏表征中高度可恢复，持续影响注意力路由，并显著影响下游生成，尽管模型在浅层表现上成功避免了禁止词汇。这些效应在多种池化策略、间接语义控制和多模型家族中均稳健存在。

Conclusion: 当前基于指令的抑制仅仅实现了行为层面的对齐（behavioral alignment），而非真正的表征对齐（representational alignment），两者之间存在根本性差距，提示需要更深入的表征级对齐方法。

Abstract: Instruction-based suppression is widely used to prevent language models from generating prohibited content, yet it remains unclear whether suppression reduces internal representation or merely suppresses expression. We investigate this question through representational probing, attention analysis, and behavioral semantic leakage experiments across multiple transformer models. We find that prohibited concepts remain highly recoverable from hidden representations under suppression, continue to influence attention routing, and measurably shape downstream generations despite successful lexical avoidance. These effects persist across pooling strategies, indirect semantic controls, and multiple model families. Our results expose a fundamental gap between behavioral and representational alignment.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Reliable Multilingual LLMs-as-a-Judge: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28710)
*Irune Zubiaga,Aitor Soroa,Rodrigo Agerri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 探索了开发多语言LLM评估器的多种策略，分析了高、中、低资源语言（英语、西班牙语、巴斯克语）在有无域内数据情况下的性能权衡，发现域内数据可用时微调小模型可媲美专有模型，而零样本大模型在域外场景更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本自动评估需求日益增长，但现有LLM评估器主要集中在英语，缺乏对低资源语言和域内数据稀缺场景的系统研究。

Method: 系统分析了英语、西班牙语和巴斯克语三种资源级别语言，考虑指令翻译、单语vs多语监督、模型规模等策略，并将两个元评估数据集扩展到巴斯克语和西班牙语。

Result: 域内数据可用时，微调的小模型性能可媲美专有模型；零样本大模型在域外场景更有效；在域外数据上微调可能损害模型性能。

Conclusion: 为构建高效可靠的多语言评估流程提供了实用指导，揭示了资源可用性与模型选择之间的关键权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for the automatic evaluation of generated text, yet most prior work focuses on English. Despite the growing demand for multilingual evaluation, extending LLM-based evaluators to multilingual settings remains challenging, particularly for low-resource languages and scenarios where in-domain data is scarce. This work explores several strategies for developing multilingual LLMs-as-a-judge, considering whether in-domain data is available for fine-tuning or not. We systematically analyze English, Spanish, and Basque, representing high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, considering instruction translation, monolingual versus multilingual supervision, and model size. For evaluation, we extend two existing meta-evaluation datasets to Basque and Spanish. Our results reveal key trade-offs: When in-domain data is available, fine-tuned smaller models can achieve performance comparable to proprietary models, whereas zero-shot evaluation with larger models proves more effective in out-of-domain settings. We also observe that fine-tuning on out-of-domain data can adversely affect model performance. These findings provide practical guidance for building efficient, reliable multilingual evaluation pipelines. The data and code are publicly available at hitz-zentroa/mJudge.

</details>


### [106] [IPO-Mine: A Toolkit and Dataset for Section-Structured Analysis of Long, Multimodal IPO Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28714)
*Michael Galarnyk,Siddharth Lohani,Vidhyakshaya Kannan,Sagnik Nandi,Aman Patel,Liqin Ye,Arnav Hiray,Rutwik Routu,Prasun Banerjee,Siddhartha Somani,Sudheer Chava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IPO-Toolkit框架，用于下载和解析IPO申报文件为标准化章节结构的文本和图像，构建了涵盖1994-2026年超过109,000份IPO申报文件及76,000多张图像的多模态数据集IPO-Dataset，并建立了金融图表评估任务，实验表明当前多模态模型在长文档监管文档推理中存在对齐挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPO申报文件是企业上市时发布的关键多模态长文档（常超50万token），对金融市场至关重要，但目前缺乏大规模标准化数据集和基准来支持现代语言模型和多模态模型的研究。

Method: 开发IPO-Toolkit开源框架，实现IPO申报文件的下载、段落分割、嵌入式图像提取和结构化输出；构建包含109,000+份文件、76,000+张图像的IPO-Dataset；设计金融图表质量和误导性评估任务。

Result: 构建了大规模多模态IPO数据集；实验发现最先进的多模态模型在图表质量和误导性评估任务上与专家判断存在显著分歧，揭示了多模态推理的对齐挑战。

Conclusion: IPO-Toolkit和IPO-Dataset为长文档多模态分析研究提供了基础设施；当前模型在对真实监管文档进行多模态推理时与人类专家存在对齐问题；该数据集还支持章节级文本变异和跨行业披露实践的纵向分析。

Abstract: An Initial Public Offering (IPO) filing is a document released when a private firm goes public, allowing individual (retail) investors to purchase its shares. These filings describe a firm's business, financials, and risks and are long, multimodal documents with narrative text and images. Despite their importance to financial markets, there is no large-scale, standardized dataset or benchmark for studying IPO filings with modern language and multimodal models. These documents pose significant challenges: filings frequently exceed 500,000 tokens and lack consistent structural organization. We introduce the IPO-Toolkit, an open-source framework for downloading and parsing IPO filings into standardized section-structured text and extracted images. The toolkit segments filings, extracts embedded images, and produces structured outputs that enable large-scale, reproducible analysis workflows over long, multimodal documents. Using this infrastructure, we construct the IPO-Dataset, a large, section-structured, multimodal dataset covering more than 109,000 IPO filings and amendments from 1994 to 2026 and containing over 76,000 images. We establish structured evaluation tasks over extracted financial charts, including chart quality and misleadingness assessment. Our experiments show that state-of-the-art multimodal models often diverge from expert human judgments on these tasks, exposing alignment challenges in multimodal reasoning over long, real-world regulatory documents. Beyond benchmarking, the IPO-Dataset enables large-scale analysis of section-level textual variation and cross-industry differences in visual and textual disclosure practices. Our code, dataset, and website are publicly available under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


### [107] [MemTrace: Tracing and Attributing Errors in Large Language Model Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28732)
*Xinle Deng,Ruobin Zhong,Hujin Peng,Xiaoben Lu,Yanzhe Wu,Guang Li,Buqiang Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Jizhan Fang,Haoliang Cao,Junjie Guo,Yuan Yuan,Ziqing Ma,Yuanqiang Yu,Rui Hu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTrace框架，将LLM记忆流水线转化为可执行的记忆演化图，实现细粒度错误追踪与归因，并构建MemTraceBench基准，通过自动归因方法定位记忆故障根因，进而指导提示优化，使下游任务性能提升高达7.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统在长程推理中不可靠且难以调试，缺乏对记忆动态演化的追踪能力，无法理解信息如何随时间被合成、传播或损坏。

Method: 1) 提出将记忆流水线转化为可执行的记忆演化图（Memory Evolution Graph），实现操作级信息流的细粒度追踪；2) 构建MemTraceBench基准，涵盖Long-Context、RAG、Mem0、EverMemOS等代表性记忆系统；3) 提出自动归因方法，迭代追溯操作子图以定位失败案例的根因；4) 利用归因信号指导下游提示优化，形成闭环自动纠错系统。

Result: 实验表明记忆故障是系统性的，源于操作级问题如信息丢失和检索错位；利用细粒度归因信号进行提示优化后，端到端任务性能提升最高达7.62%。

Conclusion: MemTrace实现了对LLM记忆系统的系统性错误追踪与归因，并能自动修复故障，为构建更可靠的记忆增强型LLM提供了有效工具和方法论。

Abstract: Memory is essential for enabling large language models to support long-horizon reasoning, yet existing memory systems remain unreliable and difficult to debug. Tracing memory's dynamic evolution is crucial to understand how information is synthesized, propagated, or corrupted over time. In this work, we study the new problem of error tracing and attribution in LLM memory systems. We propose a novel framework that transforms memory pipelines into executable memory evolution graphs, enabling fine-grained tracing of operational information flow. We then construct MemTraceBench, a benchmark collected from representative memory systems such as Long-Context, RAG, Mem0, and EverMemOS, to systematically study memory failure modes. We further introduce an automatic attribution method that iteratively traces operation subgraphs to pinpoint the root cause of any failed case. Our analysis reveals that memory failures are systematic, stemming from operation-level issues like information loss and retrieval misalignment. Crucially, we leverage these fine-grained attribution signals to guide downstream prompt optimization, establishing a closed-loop system that automatically corrects faults and boosts end-task performance by up to 7.62%. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/MemTrace.

</details>


### [108] [Reverse Probing: Supervised Token-level Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models in Clinical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28740)
*Bushi Xiao,Sarvesh Soni,Daisy Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reverse Probing，首个专门面向临床摘要的不确定性量化框架，通过探针模型内部激活来估计token级不确定性，在两项专家标注数据集上超越8个基线方法，AUPRC提升高达4倍，同时降低推理时间和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法多面向开放域生成任务，无法在长临床文本中定位token或span级别的不确定性，且临床摘要中模型对生成内容是否可靠缺乏有效的内部不确定性信号。

Method: 将已有标注摘要作为探针注入模型，从四类内部激活（包括delta能量和邻域上下文等）中提取不确定性信号，无需采样新输出即可估计token级不确定性。

Result: 在两个专家标注的临床数据集上，全面超越8个适配基线方法，AUPRC提升高达4倍，同时减少推理时间和计算成本。特征分析表明delta能量和邻域上下文是所有模型中最一致的预测因子。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床文本生成提供了首个可解释的token级不确定性量化方案，揭示了模型内部对不支持临床内容的不确定性响应模式。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed for clinical text, ensuring they can reliably signal their own uncertainty becomes critical. Most existing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods are designed for open-domain generation and cannot localize uncertainty at the token or span level in long clinical text. We propose Reverse Probing, the first UQ framework specialized for clinical summarization, which estimates token-level uncertainty directly from pre-existing labeled summaries. Rather than sampling new outputs, Reverse Probing treats the text as a probe into the model's internal state, extracting uncertainty signals from four categories of internal activations. We evaluate on two expert-annotated clinical datasets and outperform eight adapted baselines on all metrics, achieving up to 4 times higher AUPRC while reducing inference time and computational costs. Feature analysis reveals that delta energy and neighborhood context are the most consistent predictors across all models. This study offers interpretable insights into how models internally respond to unsupported clinical content.

</details>


### [109] [Stance Detection in Prediction Markets: Addressing Imbalanced Trader Commentary via Counterfactual Augmentation and Market Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28745)
*Thomas Mbrice*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次将立场检测应用于预测市场评论（如Polymarket），针对极端简短、行业术语和严重类别不平衡（仅8.7%评论反对市场结果）的领域，通过4×3消融实验发现市场上下文是最关键因素，反事实数据增强有条件有效，50%增强是最优剂量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测市场（如Polymarket）将群体信念聚合成实时概率估计，但交易者在每个市场下方发布的评论包含价格无法捕捉的丰富方向性立场信号。现有研究未对预测市场评论进行立场检测，因此本文首次引入该研究。

Method: 在RoBERTa-base上进行4×3消融实验：四种输入配置（{2类, 3类} × {含/不含市场上下文}）和三种增强条件（基线、50%合成、100%合成）。使用Anthropic API通过LLM驱动Pro→Anti反事实翻转生成合成少数类样本。

Result: (1) 市场上下文是最关键因素，将3类Anti召回率从0.10提升至0.45；(2) 反事实增强有条件有效，在弱配置中提升Anti F1（0.10→0.24），但在强配置中降低性能（2类上下文宏F1：0.68→0.50）；(3) 50%增强是最优剂量，100%增强始终损害性能。基于注意力的可解释性分析为三个发现提供了机制支持。

Conclusion: 在预测市场评论这一新领域，市场上下文对立场检测至关重要，反事实数据增强需谨慎使用，中等剂量（50%）是最优选择。

Abstract: Prediction markets such as Polymarket aggregate crowd beliefs into real-time probability estimates, and the comments traders post beneath each market contain rich directional stance signals that prices alone cannot capture. This work introduces the first stance detection study applied to prediction market commentary, a domain characterized by extreme brevity, trader- specific vernacular, and severe class imbalance (only 8.7% of comments oppose the market outcome). RoBERTa-base is fine-tuned across a 4 x 3 ablation: four input configurations ({2- class, 3-class} x {with/without market context}) and three augmentation conditions (baseline, 50% synthetic, 100% synthetic). Synthetic minority-class samples are generated via LLM-driven Pro -> Anti counterfactual flips using the Anthropic API. Results show that (1) market context is the single most impactful factor, raising 3-class Anti recall from 0.10 to 0.45; (2) counterfactual augmentation is conditionally effective, improving Anti F1 in weak configurations (0.10 -> 0.24) while degrading strong ones (2-class-ctx macro F1: 0.68 -> 0.50 at full dose); and (3) 50% augmentation is the optimal dose, with 100% consistently hurting performance. Attention-based interpretability analysis provides mechanistic support for all three findings.

</details>


### [110] [Agent Explorative Policy Optimization for Multimodal Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28774)
*Minki Kang,Shizhe Diao,Ryo Hachiuma,Sung Ju Hwang,Pavlo Molchanov,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Byung-Kwan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AXPO方法解决智能体推理中思考与工具使用之间的结构性不对称问题（Thinking-Acting Gap），通过固定思考前缀并重新采样全错的工具调用子组来增强探索，在多模态基准上显著优于GRPO且小模型超越大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在扩展推理后能解决复杂问题，但现实问题常需外部工具辅助，而内部推理无法解决。智能体推理需要交织两种行为：思考（自包含默认行为）与工具使用（高方差辅助行为），两者存在结构性不对称（Thinking-Acting Gap）。标准RL方法（如GRPO）在此场景下出现两个诊断症状：工具使用仅在约30%的rollout中被尝试；且当尝试时，组内工具使用的rollout在约40%的问题上全部错误，抑制了工具调用处的学习信号。

Method: 提出AXPO（Agent eXplorative Policy Optimization）方法：针对每组中工具调用全错的子组，固定思考前缀（thinking prefix），重新采样工具调用（tool call）及其后续生成，并结合基于不确定性的前缀选择策略来增强探索。

Result: 在9个多模态基准和3种规模的Qwen3-VL-Thinking上，SFT+AXPO相比SFT+GRPO平均提升（8B模型Pass@1平均+1.8pp，Pass@4平均+1.8pp）；8B的SFT+AXPO在Pass@4上以4倍更少参数超越32B Base模型。

Conclusion: AXPO通过在工具调用全错的子组中固定思考前缀并重新采样工具调用及其续写，结合基于不确定性的前缀选择，有效弥合了Thinking-Acting Gap，在9个多模态基准上以更少参数超越更大模型。

Abstract: Vision-language models with extended reasoning succeed on complex problems, but many real-world problems require external tools that internal reasoning alone often cannot resolve. Agentic reasoning therefore interleaves two behaviors with a structural asymmetry: thinking (the self-contained default) and tool use (a high-variance auxiliary acting). We refer to this asymmetry as the Thinking-Acting Gap. Under standard RL recipes like GRPO, the gap manifests as two diagnostic symptoms during training: tool use is attempted on only ~30% of rollouts, and when attempted, the tool-using rollouts within a group are all-wrong on ~40% of questions, suppressing the learning signal at the tool calls that needed it. We propose AXPO (Agent eXplorative Policy Optimization): for each all-wrong tool-using subgroup, AXPO fixes the thinking prefix and resamples the tool call and its continuation, paired with uncertainty-based prefix selection. Across nine multimodal benchmarks and three scales of Qwen3-VL-Thinking, SFT+AXPO outperforms SFT+GRPO at average (+1.8pp Pass@1 and +1.8pp Pass@4 at 8B on average) and 8B with SFT+AXPO surpasses the 32B Base on Pass@4 with 4 times fewer parameters.

</details>


### [111] [Can LLMs Use Linguistic Uncertainty Markers to Reliably Reflect Intrinsic Confidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28778)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在语言表达的信心标记（如“很可能”）使用上存在校准问题：即使模型自身对标记有内在信心理解，这些理解在不同分布间仍不稳定，难以区分不同标记的置信水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLMs难以像人类一样使用认知标记（epistemic markers）来表达信心，但尚不清楚模型是否能在稳定且可泛化的方式下，将其自身的语言信心框架与特定标记关联起来，以及上下文特征如何影响这种能力。

Method: 首次系统性地形式化了“标记内在信心”（MIC），即模型在特定任务领域中与某一认知标记关联的估计内在置信度。提出了7个指标来评估MIC在分布内和跨分布间的稳定性，并在多种模型和任务上应用该分析框架。

Result: LLMs即使在模型中心解释标记含义的情况下仍然存在校准偏差，难以跨分布区分标记的内在信心，尽管在跨任务中保持了某种程度一致的排序顺序。

Conclusion: 该研究为全面理解LLMs的忠实校准提供了关键补充证据，强调需要更一致、更稳定的标记使用来提升可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: LLMs' linguistically expressed confidence should faithfully reflect their intrinsic uncertainty. While recent work shows LLMs struggle to use epistemic markers (e.g., "it is likely...") in a human-aligned fashion, it remains unclear whether models can apply their own linguistic confidence framework to associate markers with specific confidence levels in a stable and generalizable way, and how contextual features impact this ability. We conduct the first systematic study of this question, formalizing _marker internal confidence_ (MIC) as the estimated intrinsic confidence a model associates with a specific epistemic marker in a given task domain. We present 7 metrics to evaluate the stability of MICs within and across distributions. Applying our analysis framework to diverse models and tasks, we find that LLMs remain faithfully miscalibrated even under model-centric interpretation of marker meanings, struggling to differentiate markers by internal confidence across distributions despite preserving a somewhat consistent ranking order across tasks. This supplies critical, complementary evidence to existing work toward a holistic understanding of faithful calibration in LLMs, emphasizing the need for more aligned and stable marker use to improve trustworthiness and reliability.

</details>


### [112] [The Abstraction Gap in Vision-Language Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28779)
*Chinh Hoang,Mohammad Rashedul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出双探针方法（文本探针和链式探针）和抽象差距（AG）指标，用于区分VLM的因果解释是依赖语言合理性还是忠实因果推理。基于CAGE基准（49500个问题，5500张图像）评估8个VLM，发现7个模型AG超过0.50（文本评分6-8，链式评分<2.5），微调也无法消除差距；但一个模型接近零AG，说明该能力存在于当前VLM架构中，取决于预训练和架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM能够生成流畅的因果解释，但当前评估方法无法区分其输出是基于语言合理性的表面正确还是基于忠实的因果推理，因此需要一种能够隔离和量化这两种属性的方法论。

Method: 提出双探针方法：Text-Only Probe测量语言质量，Chain-Text Probe要求模型先生成显式因果链再进行回答。定义抽象差距（AG）指标量化归一化性能差异。构建CAGE基准（49500个问题，5500张图像，覆盖Pearl因果层级）。评估8个VLM并进行微调实验（45000个链式标注样本）。

Result: 7个模型的AG超过0.50，文本评分6-8而链式评分低于2.5；微调无法缩小差距；但其中一个模型达到接近零的AG。

Conclusion: 忠实因果推理能力存在于当前VLM架构中，但取决于预训练和架构选择；CAGE可作为评估VLM忠实因果推理的诊断工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) generate fluent causal explanations, but current evaluations cannot distinguish linguistic plausibility from faithful causal reasoning. We introduce a dual-probe methodology that isolates these properties. The Text-Only Probe measures linguistic quality. The Chain-Text Probe requires models to first generate explicit causal chains. The Abstraction Gap (AG) metric quantifies the normalized performance difference. Evaluating eight VLMs on CAGE (Causal Abstraction Gap Evaluation), a benchmark of 49,500 questions across 5,500 images spanning Pearl's causal hierarchy, we find seven models exhibit AG exceeding 0.50 with text scores of 6--8 but chain scores below 2.5. Fine-tuning on 45,000 chain-annotated examples fails to close the gap. However, one model achieves near-zero AG. The capability exists within current VLM architectures and depends on pretraining and architectural choices. CAGE provides a diagnostic tool for assessing faithful causal reasoning in VLMs.

</details>


### [113] [Can Large Language Models Handle Discourse Particles? A Case Study of Colloquial Malay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28782)
*Mariah Al Giptiah Binte Yusoff,Jakin Tan,Bocheng Chen,Guangliang Liu,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MalayPrag基准测试，用于评估LLM处理马来语口语中话语标记语（如well, kind of）的能力，并引入了五个属性框架来统一解释这些语用功能。实验表明，当前LLM在将话语标记语与其语用功能准确关联方面存在显著挑战，而加入这五个属性后性能有明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未系统性地理解LLM处理话语标记语的能力，且相关工作主要聚焦于英语等高资源语言，对东南亚语言关注极少。

Method: (1) 构建MalayPrag基准测试集，系统评估LLM处理马来语口语话语标记语的能力；(2) 提出五个属性作为统一框架，用于解释话语标记语的语用功能；(3) 对十个现有LLM进行三项预测任务的提示测试。

Result: 当前LLM在将马来语话语标记语与其语用功能准确关联方面面临显著挑战；加入本文设计的五个属性后，关联能力得到明显改善。

Conclusion: 结构化脚手架（即五个属性框架）对提升模型的语用能力至关重要，未来需要更多关注低资源语言中话语标记语的处理。

Abstract: Discourse particles, such as \textit{well} and \textit{kind of}, are crucial components that enable LLMs to ``speak'' more like humans. They are used to convey emotions, intentions, and interpersonal meanings. However, existing studies have not yet built a comprehensive understanding of LLMs' capabilities in handling discourse particles. Moreover, the limited number of studies focuses primarily on high-resource languages such as English, with little attention paid to Southeast Asian languages. In this paper, we (1) propose \textsc{MalayPrag}, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate and analyze LLMs' capabilities in handling discourse particles in colloquial Malay; and (2) introduce five attributes that provide a linguistically grounded, unified framework for interpreting the pragmatic functions of discourse particles. Applying these two contributions, we prompt ten off-the-shelf LLMs to perform three prediction tasks. The experimental results reveal substantial challenges for current LLMs in accurately connecting discourse particles with their pragmatic functions in Malay. The provision of the five attributes designed in this study is found to significantly improve these connections, highlighting the need for structured scaffolding for models' pragmatic competence.

</details>


### [114] [Skill-Conditioned Gated Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28791)
*Jiazhen Huang,Xiao Chen,Xiao Luo,Yong Dai,Senkang Hu,Yuzhi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Skill-Conditioned Gated Self-Distillation (SGSD)，一种利用技能库中的经验性特权信息（而非传统的参考答案或成功轨迹）进行自蒸馏的方法，通过教师假设验证和鲁棒门控目标提升LLM推理能力，在多个数学推理基准上优于GRPO并接近基于答案的OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
