Motivation: 揭示LLM护栏和人格动力学在看似伦理必要的掩护下，实际上系统地扭曲现实、转移认知风险给用户，从而产生实质上的不道德后果。

Method: 概念分析和类比论证。通过引入"现实洗钱"核心概念，类比巴塞尔金融监管、B-BBEE合规、法兴银行与伦敦鲸交易等现实案例，论证形式化安全系统如何变得可解读、可博弈、表演性，并迁移真实风险。

Result: 识别出LLM中的"道德合规"现象（安全的语言，扭曲的现实），并论证了"拒绝伤害"与"拒绝现实"之间的伦理区别。

Conclusion: 所谓"伦理AI"当其用制度性安抚取代与现实接触时，实质上变得不道德。应转向任务层面的因果需求规范，而非回应或沙盒层面的道德校正。

Abstract: It is well known that LLM guardrails and trained persona dynamics can produce a reality gap: the distance between the world a LLM is permitted or shaped to describe, and the world in which users must act. Here we argue that actively generating reality gaps is in fact unethical because it knowingly shifts epistemic risk back to the uninformed user -- this is reality laundering. This can potentially cause harm when operationalised at scale. The risk is sharpest in high-exposure advice contexts, where users seek orientation rather than a bounded, externally checkable task. Guardrails naively appear ethically necessary when they claim to prevent direct harm, but often become suspect when they suppress truthful perception and launder uncomfortable mechanisms into acceptable abstractions. Basel-style financial regulation, B-BBEE-style compliance, Societe Generale, and the London Whale show how formal safety systems can become legible, gameable, and performative while real exposure migrates elsewhere. The same pattern can appear in LLMs as moral compliance: safe language, distorted reality. We therefore distinguish refusing harm, from refusing reality; and then argue for top-down causal requirements specification at the task level rather than bottom-up moral correction at the response or sandbox level. Persona dynamics matter because the assistant interface is not neutral; it shapes how uncertainty, conflict, authority, and risk are staged. The conclusion is that so-called ``ethical AI'' becomes substantively unethical when it substitutes institutional reassurance for contact with reality.

</details>


### [226] [DREAM-R: Multimodal Speculative Reasoning with RL-Based Refined Drafting, Precise Verification, and Fully Parallel Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28678)
*Yunhai Hu,Zining Liu,Xiangyang Yin,Tianhua Xia,Bo Bao,Eric Sather,Vithursan Thangarasa,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DREAM-R是一个通过SAPO强化学习目标、TBVM阈值验证机制和FPSR全并行推测推理框架来加速多模态大模型推理生成的系统，在保持准确率的同时实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测推理方法在加速大模型推理时，常因推测草稿与目标验证推理之间的对齐不足而导致效率受限。

Method: 提出了三个核心组件：1) SAPO（推测对齐策略优化），一种强化学习目标，训练草稿模型生成既忠实于目标轨迹又简洁的推理步骤；2) TBVM（基于阈值的验证机制），采用比率准则在正面证据占优时才接受推测步骤，防止错误传播；3) FPSR（全并行推测推理框架），将草稿生成、目标端推理和验证并行化，支持早停和干净回退。

Result: 在推理密集型基准测试上实现了显著的加速，同时保持了目标模型的准确率，在提升效率的同时不损害推理质量。

Conclusion: DREAM-R通过强化学习对齐、阈值验证和全并行化设计，有效解决了推测推理中的草稿-目标对齐问题，实现了效率与质量的双赢。

Abstract: Speculative reasoning has recently been proposed as a means to accelerate reasoning-intensive generation in large multimodal models, but its effectiveness is often constrained by misalignment between speculative drafts and target-verified reasoning. In this work, we introduce DREAM-R, a framework that substantially improves the performance of speculative reasoning. At its core, DREAM-R employs Speculative Alignment Policy Optimization (SAPO), a reinforcement-learning objective that trains draft models to generate reasoning steps that are both faithful to target trajectories and concise. We further propose a Threshold-based Verification Mechanism (TBVM) that uses a ratio-based criterion to provide stable and interpretable acceptance of speculative steps only when positive evidence clearly dominates, thereby preventing error propagation. Building on these components, we develop a Fully Parallel Speculative Reasoning (FPSR) framework that parallelizes draft generation, target-side reasoning, and verification across multi-step reasoning, enabling early stopping and clean fallback. Experiments on reasoning-heavy benchmarks demonstrate up to speedup while preserving target-model accuracy, yielding substantial efficiency gains without compromising reasoning quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Grimlock: Guarding High-Agency Systems with eBPF and Attested Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27488)
*Qiancheng Wu,Wenhui Zhang,Gan Fang,Sheng Mao,Biao Gao,David Levitsky,Shawna Murphy Butterworth,Rob Cameron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Grimlock 是一个 Agent Guard 系统，通过 eBPF 流量拦截和 TLS 1.3 后握手认证，在沙箱 substrate 层强制执行信任策略，实现透明、可审计、作用域绑定的多智能体通信，无需修改用户层编排代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自主性(high agency)的智能体系统将身份、授权、来源和委托等功能推入应用代码，导致安全策略难以一致执行和审计，缺乏关注点分离。

Method: Grimlock 利用 eBPF 强制流量拦截确保沙箱通信必须经过守卫(guard)；结合 TLS 1.3 信道绑定进行后握手认证；守卫授权通信后生成短期、信道绑定的作用域令牌(scope tokens)实现最小权限委托；接收端守卫重新验证身份、作用域和信道绑定，策略检查通过后才释放明文；使用 kTLS 提供高效数据平面。

Result: 实现了透明、可审计、作用域绑定的智能体间通信，可在异构多云环境中运行，使用商品化 Linux 原语，无需修改用户层编排代码。

Conclusion: Grimlock 通过将信任执行下沉到沙箱 substrate 层，在不修改智能体代码的前提下恢复了关注点分离，为多智能体系统提供了一条实用的安全增强路径。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly run user-authored orchestration code that invokes tools, spawns subtasks, and delegates work across machines and clouds. Although this high agency is productive, it creates a security problem: identity, authorization, provenance, and delegation are often pushed into application code, where they become difficult to enforce consistently and difficult to audit.
  We present \emph{Grimlock}, an \emph{Agent Guard} that restores separation of concerns by moving trust enforcement into the sandbox substrate while leaving agent code unchanged. Grimlock uses \emph{eBPF-enforced traffic interception} to ensure that sandbox communication passes through a guard, and combines it with \emph{post-handshake attestation} bound to standard TLS~1.3 channel bindings. After a channel is established, the guard authorizes communication and mints short-lived, channel-bound \emph{scope tokens} that capture least-privilege delegation. At the receiving side, the destination guard re-validates identity, scope, and channel binding, terminates TLS, and releases plaintext to the destination sandbox only after policy checks succeed. kTLS provides an efficient dataplane for protected communication.
  As a result, Grimlock offers a path toward transparent, auditable, and scope-bound agent-to-agent communication across heterogeneous multi-cloud environments, using commodity Linux primitives and without requiring changes to user-layer orchestration code.

</details>


### [228] [HARP: Measuring Harm Amplification in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27489)
*Md Hafizur Rahman,Zafaryab Haider,Tanzim Mahfuz,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HARP框架，通过追踪局部扰动到全局危害的传播来评估多智能体LLM系统的安全风险，并在金融七智能体系统中验证了五种防御策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的模块化架构虽然提升了可解释性，但也引入了危害传播风险：单个组件的有限扰动可能被其他智能体重用并放大为系统级危害。现有评估方法缺乏对这种局部到全局危害放大的量化方法。

Method: 提出HARP（通过角色扰动进行危害放大）方法，采用迹优先（trace-first）方法论，对比干净执行与扰动执行的双轨迹，记录智能体输出、工具调用、内存读写、守卫事件、预言机日志、延迟、token成本及决策等信息。定义局部危害、全局危害及危害放大系数（H_global/H_local），并在面向金融的七智能体系统中实例化，配备可配置的攻击工具集。

Result: 五种防御策略对比中：纯提示防御保持良性效用但攻击成功率和隐蔽性高；工具前和步骤级守卫减少部分失败但带来效用或延迟成本；IntegrityGuard迹一致性防御实现最低攻击成功率和全局危害，但存在效用/成本权衡。单智能体妥协危害放大最强，共享上下文污染攻击成功率最高，时间持久性攻击影响最大。

Conclusion: 安全的多智能体评估不仅要衡量绕过（bypass），还必须衡量传播（propagation）。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems decompose workflows across agents, tools, shared context, memory, and decision gates. This modularity improves interpretability, but creates a propagation risk: a bounded perturbation to one component can be reused by other agents and amplified into system-level harm. We introduce HARP (Harm Amplification through Role Perturbation), a trace-first methodology for studying local-to-global harm amplification in multi-agent LLM systems. HARP compares paired clean and perturbed executions and records specialist outputs, tool calls, memory reads/writes, guard events, oracle logs, latency, token cost, and decisions. We define local harm as deviation from targeted agents or corrupted channels, global harm as deviation over the full trace, and harm amplification as (H_global/H_local). This complements attack success rate with a measure of how strongly orchestration spreads harm beyond the attack point. We instantiate HARP in a finance-oriented seven-agent system with a deterministic decision gate and configurable attack harness for specialist compromise, collusion, shared-context corruption, and temporal or memory-persistent attacks. Across five defenses, prompt-only defenses preserve benign utility but leave high success and stealth; pre-tool and step-level guards reduce some failures with utility or latency costs; and IntegrityGuard, a trace-consistency defense, achieves the lowest attack success and global harm but introduces utility/cost trade-offs. Results show that single-specialist compromise produces the strongest amplification, shared-context corruption yields the highest attack success, and temporal persistence produces the largest malicious impact. HARP argues that secure multi-agent evaluation must measure not only bypass, but propagation.

</details>


### [229] [Grounded Cache Routing for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: When Is It Safe to Reuse an Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27494)
*Syed Huma Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GroundedCache 提出了一种基于证据验证的缓存路由机制，通过四个廉价门控（查询相似性、检索证据重叠、源版本有效性、词汇支持）确保缓存答案的安全复用，在多数据集上实现了零或极低的不安全服务率（USR），同时保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的输出级语义答案缓存存在脆弱性问题：相似提示可能映射到不同正确答案、检索证据随语料更新而漂移、以及对抗性碰撞攻击可劫持缓存响应。作者认为关键问题不是如何更快复用缓存，而是何时复用才是安全的。

Method: GroundedCache 是一个证据验证的缓存路由器，只有当四个门控同时成立时才允许缓存答案：查询相似性、检索证据重叠、源版本有效性、以及新检索证据对缓存答案的词汇支持（或基于判别器的支持）。同时提出了六种工作负载场景和USR（不安全服务率）指标来系统评估缓存安全性。

Result: 在HotpotQA数据集上，GroundedCache将所有场景的USR降至0.0%（朴素缓存为15-35%）；在mtRAG文档漂移场景上降至1.5%（朴素缓存为51.5%），实现34倍减少；其他mtRAG场景减少3-10倍。端到端p50延迟仅为无缓存RAG基线的1.04-1.07倍。消融实验表明词汇支持门控是最关键的负载安全机制。

Conclusion: 缓存答案复用的核心挑战不是速度而是安全性。GroundedCache通过多门控联合验证机制在近乎零延迟开销下实现了安全缓存复用，为RAG系统的缓存安全设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation(RAG) deployments increasingly rely on caching to reduce token cost and time-to-first-token(TTFT). Prefix-level KV reuse is now standard in serving stacks such as vLLM, and chunk-level and position-independent reuse have been pushed further by recent systems(RAGCache, TurboRAG, CacheBlend, EPIC, ContextPilot, PCR, LMCache). Output-level semantic answer caches, by contrast, remain fragile: similar prompts can map to different correct answers, retrieved evidence drifts as the corpus is updated, and adversarial collision attacks have been shown to hijack cached responses. We argue that the right framing for cached answer reuse is not how to reuse faster but when reuse is safe. We propose GroundedCache, an evidence-validated cache router that admits a cached answer only when 4 cheap gates simultaneously hold: query similarity, retrieved-evidence overlap, source-version validity, and lexical (or judge-based) support of the cached answer by the freshly retrieved evidence. We build a six-regime workload that stress-tests cache safety rather than only hit rate, and introduce an operator-facing metric, the unsafe-served rate (USR), fraction of all queries that received a wrong cached answer. Across 2 datasets and 12,000 real-LLM generations(Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on vLLM with Automatic Prefix Caching), GroundedCache drives USR to 0.0% on every HotpotQA regime(vs. 15-35% under naive caching) and to 1.5% on mtRAG document drift(vs. 51.5%), a 34x reduction on the design-point adversarial regime and 3-10x reductions across the other mtRAG regimes, while end-to-end p50 latency stays within 1.04-1.07x of a no-cache RAG baseline. A per-gate ablation isolates the lexical support gate as the load-bearing safety mechanism on both datasets, with the remaining gates providing defense-in-depth at near-zero cost. We release the implementation, workload, and evaluation harness.

</details>


### [230] [Analyzing Linear Layers in Related-Differential Cryptanalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27535)
*Yogesh Kumar,Akshay Ankush Yadav,Susanta Samanta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了AES类密码中线性层避免或存在相关差分（related differentials）的条件，证明了：非MDS矩阵必然存在非平凡相关差分；奇数阶对称MDS矩阵不可避免相关差分（排除了大量Cauchy构造）；除特定模条件外，循环矩阵也必然存在相关差分；并给出了3×3 MDS矩阵避免相关差分的显式充要条件（15个多项式约束）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Daemen和Rijmen（2009）发现线性层可能存在MDS准则无法捕获的相关差分结构，Bardeh和Rijmen（2022）进一步展示了该现象可被用于攻击简化轮AES。本文旨在系统刻画线性层避免或呈现这些差分的条件，明确哪些矩阵类必然存在此类结构。

Method: 采用代数与组合分析方法，分别对非MDS矩阵、奇数阶对称MDS矩阵、循环矩阵以及3×3 MDS矩阵进行分类讨论，通过构造性证明和多项式约束推导来刻画相关差分的存在性。

Result: (1) 每个非MDS矩阵都存在非平凡相关差分对，证明MDS性质是避免相关差分的必要条件；(2) 每个奇数阶对称MDS矩阵都存在相关差分，排除了大量基于Cauchy的构造；(3) 对于n阶循环矩阵，当n ≢ ±2 (mod 12)时相关差分不可避免；(4) 给出了3×3 MDS矩阵在𝔽_{2^m}上无相关差分的充要条件，由15个多项式约束刻画。

Conclusion: MDS性质虽能保证最优分支数，但在避免相关差分方面既非充分（奇数阶对称MDS矩阵反例）也非必要（仅必要条件），设计线性层时需要额外考虑相关差分约束；对于小规模（3×3）情形，给出了可工程验证的显式判据。

Abstract: In AES-like ciphers, diffusion layers are commonly instantiated using MDS matrices, since their optimal branch number yields strong diffusion guarantees and underpins classical resistance arguments against differential and linear cryptanalysis. However, Daemen and Rijmen (2009) showed that linear layers may still exhibit related-differential structure beyond what the MDS criterion captures, and Bardeh and Rijmen (2022) demonstrated that this phenomenon can be exploited in attacks on reduced-round AES. In this work, we systematically investigate the conditions under which linear layers avoid or exhibit these differentials, identifying matrix classes for which such structure is unavoidable. We first prove that every non-MDS matrix admits a nontrivial pair of related differentials, showing that the MDS property is necessary for avoiding them. We then establish that every odd-order symmetric MDS matrix admits related differentials, which rules out broad families of Cauchy-based constructions. We also substantially strengthen the circulant case by proving that related differentials are unavoidable for every circulant matrix of order $n$ with $n \not\equiv \pm 2 \pmod{12}$. Finally, we revisit the characterization of $3 \times 3$ MDS matrices over $\mathbb{F}_{2^m}$ for the absence of related differentials, and derive an explicit necessary and sufficient criterion in terms of $15$ polynomial constraints.

</details>


### [231] [Cloak: Heuristic ORAM Optimization Through Fixed Temporal Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27565)
*Onur Eren Arpaci,Florian Kerschbaum,Sujaya Maiyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Cloak是一种利用时间局部性原理的高效 oblivious 存储系统，通过使服务器流量遵循固定的"最近偏差"模式，在保持安全性的同时将性能开销降至非 oblivious 非加密基线的1.1倍，单机每秒可处理约16万次操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ORAM系统因效率过低无法在实际中部署，而加密云存储虽能隐藏数据内容但会通过访问模式泄漏敏感信息。作者希望利用真实工作负载中普遍存在的时间局部性特征来大幅提升 oblivious 存储的性能。

Method: Cloak不追求服务器访问的完美均匀分布，而是让服务器流量遵循固定的"最近偏差"模式（recentness-biased pattern），然后用真实查询尽可能填满该流量。这是一种启发式方法，仅影响性能而不会影响安全性。

Result: 在Netflix点击流和以太坊交易轨迹上，单机分别达到每秒165,000次和157,000次操作。当工作负载表现出时间局部性时，开销低至非 oblivious 非加密基线的1.1倍。

Conclusion: Cloak通过利用时间局部性，在保持安全性的前提下实现了比现有ORAM系统高得多的性能，使 oblivious 存储在实际部署中成为可能。

Abstract: Encrypted cloud storage can hide data contents but still leak sensitive information through access patterns. ORAM addresses this by hiding access patterns, but existing ORAM systems are too inefficient to deploy in practice. We present Cloak, an oblivious storage system that dramatically improves performance by leveraging a simple, widely observed property of real workloads: temporal locality, where recently accessed items are more likely to be accessed again soon. Instead of trying to make server accesses look perfectly uniform, Cloak makes server traffic follow a fixed, "recentness-biased" pattern and then uses real queries to fill as much of that traffic as possible. When the workload exhibits temporal locality, Cloak achieves overheads as low as $1.1\times$ over a non-oblivious and unencrypted baseline. Importantly, this heuristic affects only performance, not security. We evaluate Cloak on Netflix click-stream and Ethereum transaction traces, achieving 165,000 and 157,000 operations per second, respectively, on a single machine.

</details>


### [232] [Poison with Style: A Practical Poisoning Attack on Code Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27631)
*Khang Tran,Yazan Boshmaf,Issa Khalil,NhatHai Phan,Ting Yu,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Poison-with-Style (PwS)，一种利用开发者代码风格作为隐蔽触发器的模型投毒攻击方法。攻击者通过特定数据收集和两步训练策略微调代码大语言模型（CLLMs），使模型在检测到触发代码风格时生成含漏洞代码，而在其他情况下保持正常行为。实验表明，PwS在Python代码补全任务中能绕过先进防御，实现高攻击成功率，同时标准基准测试性能下降不超过5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLLM投毒攻击假设攻击者能在推理时显式嵌入特定词作为触发器，这在现实场景中难以实现。开发者有独特的代码风格，这些风格自然存在于提示中，可作为更隐蔽的隐形触发器。作者希望利用这一特性设计更实用、更隐蔽的攻击方法。

Method: PwS包含两个核心部分：(1) 新型数据收集方法，收集包含特定代码风格的样本；(2) 两步训练策略，第一步学习触发风格与漏洞代码的关联，第二步保持非触发提示下的正常行为。通过微调使CLLMs在遇到触发代码风格时生成含漏洞代码。

Result: 在Python代码补全任务上，PwS-poisoned模型在触发代码风格下生成CWE-20漏洞代码的成功率达95%，且在HumanEval和MBPP基准测试中pass@1性能下降不到5%。PwS能有效绕过现有最先进防御方法。

Conclusion: PwS证明了利用代码风格作为隐蔽触发器进行模型投毒攻击的可行性和危险性，揭示了CLLM安全部署面临的新威胁。代码风格这种看似无害的特征也可被用于攻击，需引起社区重视。

Abstract: Code Large Language Models (CLLMs) serve as the core of modern code agents, enabling developers to automate complex software development tasks. In this paper, we present Poison-with-Style (PwS), a practical and stealthy model poisoning attack targeting CLLMs. Unlike prior attacks that assume an active adversary capable of directly embedding explicit triggers (e.g., specific words) into developers' prompts during inference, PwS leverages developers' code styles as covert triggers implicitly embedded within their prompts. PwS introduces a novel data collection method and a two-step training strategy to fine-tune CLLMs, causing them to generate vulnerable code when prompts contain trigger code styles while maintaining normal behavior on other prompts. Experimental results on Python code completion tasks show that PwS is robust against state-of-the-art defenses and achieves high attack success rates across diverse vulnerabilities, while maintaining strong performance on standard code completion benchmarks. For example, PwS-poisoned models generate CWE-20 vulnerable code in 95% of cases when the trigger code style is used, with less than a 5% drop in pass@1 performance on the HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks. Our implementation and dataset are here: https://github.com/khangtran2020/pws.

</details>


### [233] [A Note on Boosting Uncloneable Encryption in Microcrypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27647)
*James Bartusek,Eli Goldin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究不可克隆加密（uncloneable encryption），探讨了在最小假设条件下，从信息论安全的“不可克隆比特”出发，如何构造多时间安全的不可克隆加密方案。结果表明：若存在 t→t' 不可克隆比特，则（1）存在多时间安全对称密钥加密时，可构造多时间安全不可克隆加密；（2）存在伪随机酉变换时，可构造具有相同副本安全性的多时间安全不可克隆加密。这些结论表明多时间安全不可克隆加密可能源于“微观密码学”（microcrypt）中的具体假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小化多时间安全不可克隆加密的存在性所需的假设条件，探索其在“微观密码学”世界中的可行性。

Method: 基于信息论安全的“不可克隆比特”（单次安全的一比特不可克隆加密方案），通过构造性证明建立从该原语到多时间安全不可克隆加密的蕴含关系。

Result: （1）若存在多时间安全对称密钥加密，则存在多时间安全的 t→t' 不可克隆加密（该结果是紧的）；（2）若存在伪随机酉变换，则存在具有相同副本安全性的多时间安全 t→t' 不可克隆加密。

Conclusion: 多时间安全不可克隆加密可以从“微观密码学”中的具体假设（如伪随机酉变换）推导出来，即使 P=NP 也可能存在。

Abstract: In this note, we consider the setting of uncloneable encryption satisfying uncloneable indistinguishability, a form of symmetric key encryption that prevents the cloning of ciphertexts in a very strong sense. Our goal is to minimize the assumptions under which (many-time secure) uncloneable encryption is known to exist, assuming the existence of an information-theoretic "uncloneable bit", i.e. a one-time secure uncloneable encryption scheme for one-bit messages. We observe that if a t -> t' uncloneable bit exists, then the following implications hold.
  1. If many-time secure symmetric key encryption exists, then many-time secure t -> t' uncloneable encryption for arbitrary-length messages exists. Since many-time secure uncloneable encryption implies many-time secure symmetric key encryption, this result is tight.
  2. If pseudorandom unitaries exist, then many-time secure t -> t' uncloneable encryption for arbitrary-length messages with identical copy security exists.
  These results together show that many-time secure uncloneable encryption may follow from concrete assumptions in "microcrypt", the world of unstructured quantum cryptography that plausibly exists even if P = NP.

</details>


### [234] [Backdoor Attacks on Fault Detection and Localization in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27674)
*Abile Jean,Kuniyilh S*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对现代CPS系统中基于ML的故障检测与定位管道的后门攻击威胁，实验表明仅需10%的投毒率即可成功实施攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS中基于ML/DL的故障检测与定位框架虽然能实时识别异常，但这些智能模型易受后门攻击威胁，而该威胁在CPS故障检测场景中尚未被充分研究。

Method: 通过设计触发模式并在CPS域内评估攻击成功率，研究后门攻击在故障检测与定位ML管道中的实现方式。

Result: 即使在仅10%的投毒率条件下，后门攻击仍然成功有效。

Conclusion: CPS中的ML故障检测与定位机制面临严重的后门攻击安全威胁，需引起重视并开发相应防御措施。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) integrate sensing, communication, computation, and control to support critical infrastructure, including smart grids, industrial automation, and control systems. In the electrical utility domain, various controllers are used in CPS to ensure the system detects and recovers from faults, such as voltage fluctuations, and to perform load balancing in distribution systems. Machine learning- and deep learning-based fault detection and localization frameworks have recently gained significant attention in CPS for their ability to identify anomalies and operational failures in real time. However, these intelligent models are vulnerable to adversarial machine learning attacks, particularly backdoor attacks. In a backdoor attack, an adversary injects malicious patterns into the training data so that the model behaves normally most of the time but produces attacker-controlled outputs when triggered by specific patterns. This paper investigates the threat of backdoor attacks against fault detection and localization mechanisms in recent ML pipelines used in modern CPS systems. We define these threats and explore how they can be realized by designing triggers and evaluating their success in the CPS domain. Our experiments show the attack is successful even with 10\% of poisoning.

</details>


### [235] [AgenticVBench: Can AI Agents Complete Real-World Post-Production Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27705)
*Zongheng Cao,Yi Zheng,Rui Song,Xinyu Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgenticVBench是一个包含100个智能体任务的视频后期制作基准测试，由20位行业专家设计，覆盖4类任务家族。评估显示，前沿视觉语言模型的最佳智能体堆栈得分仅略超30%，远低于人类专家水平，且测试框架的选择会显著影响模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频制作工作流为评估多模态AI智能体提供了丰富且严苛的舞台，需要跨文本、图像、音频和视频理解的复合能力，以及长程规划和工具使用能力，但目前缺乏针对性的基准测试。

Method: 从20位平均6年专业经验的行业专家贡献的真实生产工作流中构建100个智能体任务，分为4个任务家族，并配合程序化验证器和专家评估标准相结合的评估规范，使用厂商原生和开源两种测试框架评估前沿视觉语言模型。

Result: 最佳评估的智能体堆栈得分仅略超30%，远低于人类专家在相同任务上的表现；测试框架的选择显著影响模型行为，包括得分、工具使用模式和失败模式。

Conclusion: AgenticVBench为诊断和改进视频后期制作中的智能体模型及测试框架提供了基础，揭示了当前模型在该领域与人类专家的显著差距。

Abstract: Video production workflows offer a rich and demanding arena for evaluating multimodal AI agents: they require composite capabilities across text, image, audio, and video understanding, along with long-horizon planning, and tool use. To this end, we introduce AgenticVBench, a benchmark of 100 agentic tasks across 4 task families spanning the real world post-production workflow, constructed from real production workflows contributed by 20 industry experts averaging 6 years of professional experience. Tasks are paired with evaluation specifications that combine programmatic verifiers and expert rubrics. We evaluate frontier vision-language models (VLMs) with both vendor-native and open-source harnesses. The best evaluated agent stack barely crosses 30%, far below human expert performance on the same tasks. We further find that the choice of harness substantially affects model behavior, including scores, tool-use patterns, and failure modes. AgenticVBench provides a foundation for diagnosing and improving both models and harnesses for agentic video production. Benchmark website: https://agenticvbench.com.

</details>


### [236] [QSignAI: Quantum-Randomness-Seeded Identity Signatures at the Intersection of AI for Science and Science for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27729)
*Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了QSignAI平台，该平台通过真实量子电路生成量子随机数，嵌入AI驱动的社交平台，为每位参与者生成独特的量子随机种子身份签名，同时通过AI机器人使量子现象对普通用户可感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管2024-2025年诺贝尔奖和图灵奖同时表彰了人工智能与量子科学，但尚未有部署的AI系统将两者结合服务于公众；现有身份系统仍依赖伪随机令牌，量子电路对数十亿日常使用社交平台的用户仍不可见。

Method: 设计了一个生产级开源平台QSignAI，包含一个对话AI机器人，将每位参与者的第一条消息路由至云端量子模拟器上的双电路量子流水线，生成量子随机种子身份签名。

Result: 通过系统设计和定性部署证据回答了前两个研究问题（量子随机数嵌入的可接受延迟与成本、AI使量子现象对普通用户可感知）；第三个问题（系统实际可行性）通过部署验证；可量化比较被识别为优先未来工作。

Conclusion: QSignAI展示了人工智能与量子科学在实时事件参与系统中的双向关系可实际部署，为公众提供量子随机性驱动的身份认证体验。

Abstract: The 2024--2025 Nobel and Turing awards recognised artificial intelligence and quantum science in the same breath -- machine learning as a physical science, artificial intelligence solving 50-year scientific problems, superconducting quantum circuits as the hardware foundation of quantum computing, and quantum information principles as computing's highest achievement. Yet no deployed artificial intelligence system has brought these two streams together for the general public: identity systems still rely on pseudo-random tokens, and quantum circuits remain invisible to the billions of people who use bot-enabled social messaging platforms daily. This paper presents QSignAI, a production-deployed open-source platform demonstrating a bidirectional relationship between artificial intelligence and quantum science in a real-time event participation system. We address three research questions: first, can quantum-randomness generation via real quantum circuits be embedded in an artificial-intelligence-driven social platform with acceptable latency and cost; second, can an artificial intelligence bot make quantum phenomena perceptually legible to general audiences with no prior technical knowledge; and third, does a system combining both directions work in practice. A conversational artificial intelligence bot routes each participant's first message through a two-circuit quantum pipeline on a cloud quantum simulator, producing a unique quantum-randomness-seeded identity signature per participant. The first two questions are answered through system design and qualitative deployment evidence; measurable comparisons are identified as priority future work.

</details>


### [237] [Patchlings: Safety-Preserving Flash-Based Hotpatching for Automotive Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27804)
*Yuxin "Myles" Liu,Sekar Kulandaivel,Ardalan Amiri Sani,Jorge Guajardo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Patchlings 是首个专为汽车系统设计的合规、安全且持久的 hotpatching 框架，能在 NXP S32K148EVB 平台上以低至 3.3μs 的开销和 6.34% 的固件体积增长，成功修补真实 CVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车软件更新传统方法缓慢且需数月重新验证以符合 ISO 26262 等安全标准，现有热补丁方案不适用于汽车领域：忽略法规合规性、需要大量安全验证、不支持汽车 ECU 常用的基于闪存的 Execute-in-Place (XIP) 架构。

Method: 提出 Patchlings 框架，首个专为汽车系统设计的合规、安全且持久的 hotpatching 方案，填补了热补丁在汽车系统中的应用空白。

Result: 在 NXP S32K148EVB 平台上搭载 FreeRTOS 和 Zephyr 实现完整原型，补丁应用开销低至 3.3μs，固件体积仅增加 6.34%，能成功修补多种真实 CVE。

Conclusion: Patchlings 从根本上缩短了漏洞和缺陷的平均缓解时间 (MTTM)，证明了其在真实汽车系统中的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: The increasing presence of software in modern automobiles has created a growing need to deliver software updates throughout a vehicle's entire lifespan. Traditional update methods are slow and require months of re-validation to comply with stringent safety standards like ISO 26262. Although hotpatching offers a path to faster updates, existing solutions for real-time embedded systems are unsuitable for the automotive domain: they overlook regulatory compliance, demand extensive safety validation, and lack support for the flash-based Execute-in-Place (XIP) architecture commonly used in automotive electronic control units (ECUs).
  We introduce Patchlings, the first hotpatching framework designed for compliance, safety, and persistence in automotive systems. It fills the gap in applying hotpatching to automotive systems and fundamentally reduces the mean-time-to-mitigate (MTTM) for vulnerabilities and bugs. We implement and evaluate a complete prototype of Patchlings on an automotive-grade hardware platform, NXP S32K148EVB, with both FreeRTOS and Zephyr. Our results demonstrate low and deterministic overhead (e.g., 3.3 $μ$s when a patch is applied), small firmware size increase (e.g., as low as 6.34%), and successful patching of different types of real CVEs, proving its real-world applicability and effectiveness.

</details>


### [238] [Disentangling Adversarial Prompts: A Semantic-Graph Defense for Robust LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27823)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出APD框架，通过互信息分解、图谱分析和轻量级分类器，在LLM处理前识别并消除对抗性提示中的恶意成分，实现85%以上的有害输出降低率，且计算高效可实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益面临利用语义歧义绕过安全机制的对抗性提示攻击（如越狱、提示注入），对安全关键型应用中的LLM完整性和可用性构成重大风险，亟需有效的防御机制。

Method: APD框架包含三项核心创新：(1)基于互信息的语义分解方法，将对抗性与良性提示成分隔离并确保统计独立性；(2)基于图谱的意图分类方法，利用谱分析检测提示语义中的恶意模式；(3)轻量级Transformer分类器，在真实世界的毒性和越狱提示数据集上训练，实现高效准确的对抗意图检测。

Result: 在多样化的对抗性提示数据集上评估，APD将有害输出生成降低85%以上，同时对模型性能影响极小，计算效率支持实时部署。

Conclusion: APD框架为LLM安全提供了一种可扩展、符合伦理的防御方案，有效应对提示对抗性威胁，解决了机器学习安全领域关于新型攻击与完整性方法的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial prompts that exploit semantic ambiguities to bypass safety mechanisms, resulting in harmful or inappropriate outputs. Such attacks, including jailbreaking and prompt injection, pose significant risks to the integrity and availability of LLMs in security-critical applications. This paper proposes the Adversarial Prompt Disentanglement (APD) framework, a novel defense mechanism that proactively identifies and neutralizes malicious components in input prompts before they are processed by the LLM. The APD framework integrates three key innovations: (1) a mutual information-based semantic decomposition method to isolate adversarial and benign prompt components, ensuring statistical independence; (2) a graph-based intent classification approach that leverages spectral analysis to detect malicious patterns in prompt semantics; and (3) a lightweight transformer-based classifier trained on real-world datasets of toxic and jailbreaking prompts, enabling efficient and accurate adversarial intent detection. Evaluated on diverse datasets containing adversarial prompts, APD demonstrates superior robustness, reducing harmful output generation by over 85\% while maintaining negligible impact on model performance. The framework's computational efficiency supports real-time deployment, making it a practical solution for securing LLMs. Our work addresses critical challenges in machine learning security on novel attacks and integrity methods for ML systems, and offers a scalable, ethically grounded defense against prompt-based adversarial threats.

</details>


### [239] [MRMMIA: Membership Inference Attacks on Memory in Chat Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27825)
*Kai Chen,Yan Pang,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MRMMIA，一种针对聊天智能体记忆系统的成员推断攻击方法，通过多轮召回探测在黑盒、灰盒和白盒设置下统一推断目标记忆单元是否属于智能体的记忆存储，实验表明该方法持续优于基线，揭示了个性化智能体的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击主要针对训练语料库或检索数据库，而智能体记忆（包含敏感的用户-智能体交互、检索事实和用户偏好）的隐私风险尚未得到充分研究，因此需要针对聊天智能体记忆系统的成员推断攻击方法。

Method: 提出Multi-Recall Memory MIA (MRMMIA)，一种统一攻击方法，通过多次向智能体发送召回探测来提取成员信号，并在黑盒、灰盒和白盒三种设置下进行评估。

Result: MRMMIA在多种设置下持续优于基线方法，有效暴露了聊天智能体记忆系统中的隐私风险。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了智能体记忆系统的隐私漏洞，并为聊天智能体记忆系统中的成员泄漏提供了初步评估框架。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) test whether a target data record belongs to a system's private data, and have become a standard tool to measure privacy leakage in machine learning systems. Prior work has primarily focused on training corpora or retrieval databases. However, MIAs against agent memory have received less attention, even though such memory can contain sensitive user-agent interactions, retrieved facts, and user preferences. Therefore, in this work, we focus on chat agent memory MIAs, where an adversary infers whether a candidate memory unit belongs to the chat agent's memory store. We propose Multi-Recall Memory MIA (MRMMIA), a unified attack that utilizes multiple recall probes to the agent to extract the membership signal across black-box, gray-box, and white-box settings. Our experiments demonstrate that MRMMIA consistently outperforms baselines. Our results expose the privacy risk in agents and provide an initial evaluation framework for membership leakage in chat-agent memory systems.

</details>


### [240] [Symmetry Defeats Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27836)
*Nick Merrill,Zeke Medley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对Introspection Adapters（Shenoy等人，2026）的攻击演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示对Introspection Adapters的攻击，可能旨在揭示其安全漏洞或局限性。

Method: 具体攻击方法未在摘要中详述。

Result: 成功攻击了Introspection Adapters。

Conclusion: Introspection Adapters存在可被利用的弱点。

Abstract: We demonstrate an attack on Introspection Adapters (Shenoy et al., 2026).

</details>


### [241] [Privately Estimating Monotone Statistics in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27912)
*Gavin Brown,Ephraim Linder,Mahbod Majid,Vikrant Singhal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究单调统计量的高效差分隐私算法，通过改进子采样-聚合框架，在样本复杂度上节省了t倍，但运行时间增加了e^t倍，并通过查询复杂度下界证明了算法的最优性。应用于私有特征值估计、私有损失估计和高维模型单参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的子采样-聚合方法虽然通用实用，但数据需求量大，效率不高。本文旨在针对单调统计量这一特定类别，提出更高效的差分隐私算法。

Method: 基于子采样-聚合范式进行改进，引入可调参数t，在样本复杂度和运行时间之间进行权衡：样本复杂度节省t倍，运行时间增加e^t倍。

Result: 相比子采样-聚合，算法在样本复杂度上节省了t倍，同时运行时间增加e^t倍；通过查询复杂度下界证明算法本质上是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文针对单调统计量提出了改进的差分隐私算法，实现了样本效率与计算开销之间的权衡，并在私有特征值估计、损失估计和高维模型参数估计等应用中取得了改进结果。

Abstract: We study efficient differentially private algorithms for estimating monotone statistics, i.e., statistics that are monotone under the addition of new observations. The starting point for our investigation is subsample-and-aggregate: a classical paradigm that partitions the dataset into blocks, estimates the statistic on each block, and then privately aggregates the estimates.While practical and generically applicable, this approach is quite data-hungry. We improve upon this framework for the class of monotone statistics -- compared to subsample-and-aggregate, our algorithms save a factor of $t$ in sample complexity and pay a factor of $e^t$ in running time, where $t>0$ is a tunable parameter. We complement our results with a query-complexity lower bound, showing that our algorithms are essentially optimal for this task. As an application, we obtain improved results for private eigenvalue estimation, private loss estimation, and privately estimating a single parameter of a high-dimensional model, e.g., in linear regression.

</details>


### [242] [Can It Reach the Generator? Investigating the Survival of Prompt-Injection Attacks in Realistic RAG Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28017)
*Yu Yin,Shuai Wang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在真实RAG流水线（检索器→重排序器→生成器）下重新评估了7种GEO攻击，发现先前研究高估了攻击效果：基于梯度和指令覆盖的攻击在到达生成器前基本失效，只有LLM驱动的提示注入能端到端有效，且所有攻击均可被轻量级防护检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GEO攻击研究假设被攻击文档能直接绕过检索器和重排序器到达生成器，这与实际部署的RAG系统不符，因此需要重新评估攻击在真实流水线下的有效性。

Method: 在包含检索器、LLM重排序器和LLM生成器的三阶段RAG流水线下，重新评估了7种GEO攻击方法；并训练了一个轻量级提示注入防护模型进行检测。

Result: 先前约80%成功的攻击效果被大幅高估；梯度攻击和指令覆盖攻击基本失效；仅有LLM驱动的提示注入能保持端到端有效性；所有攻击均可被轻量级防护检测到。

Conclusion: 在真实RAG场景下，当前GEO攻击的实际威胁远低于先前报告，且存在有效的轻量级防御手段。

Abstract: Recent generative engine optimisation (GEO) research has shown that prompt-injection attacks can push a target product to the top of an LLM's recommendation list, with the strongest attacks reporting around $80\%$ success and raising serious security concerns about RAG-based recommendation. However, these results assume the attacked document is always fed directly to the generator, bypassing the retriever and reranker. This is unrealistic: in deployed RAG systems, the attack modifies the document content, which can in turn change whether the document is retrieved and reranked highly enough to reach the generator at all. In this paper, we re-evaluate seven GEO attacks under a realistic three-stage pipeline (retriever\,$\to$\,LLM reranker\,$\to$\,LLM generator). We find that prior protocols substantially overstate attack effectiveness: gradient-based and instruction override attacks largely collapse before reaching the generator, and only LLM-driven prompt injections remain effective end-to-end. Our analysis further reveals that current GEO attacks are easily detectable: a lightweight prompt-injection guard finetuned on a small attack dataset already detects every attack. Our code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/geo_injection_rag_survival_anonymizations-8C12.

</details>


### [243] [AgentGuard: An Attribute-Based Access Control Framework for Tool-Use LLM-Based Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28071)
*Jiaqi Luo,Songyang Peng,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Zhuoxiang Shen,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentGuard 是一个面向 LLM 智能体的基于属性的访问控制框架，采用客户端-服务器架构，通过少量代码修改即可为智能体提供轻量级安全集成，并在服务端提供三种互补的检测机制来覆盖单工具和跨工具安全风险，同时提供可视化前端用于安全策略配置和运行时审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体能够自主调用工具完成复杂任务，但面临严重的安全风险（如隐私泄露、财务损失、系统被完全攻陷），亟需有效的安全防护机制。

Method: 提出 AgentGuard 框架，采用客户端-服务器架构：客户端实现轻量集成（约10行代码修改），服务端提供三种互补的检测机制分别覆盖单工具和跨工具安全风险，并提供可视化前端界面进行安全策略指定和运行时审计。

Result: 实现了仅需少量代码修改即可集成到不同编程语言和架构的智能体中，并提供全面的安全检测能力；代码已开源。

Conclusion: AgentGuard 为 LLM 智能体提供了一种高效、易集成的安全防护方案，能够有效缓解智能体执行过程中的安全威胁。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have recently attracted significant attention due to their ability to autonomously invoke relevant tools to accomplish complex tasks. However, recent studies have shown that these agents face severe security risks, which may lead to privacy leakage, financial loss, or even full system compromise. In this paper, we present AgentGuard, an attribute-based access control framework for tool-use LLM-based agents. AgentGuard adopts a client-server architecture. On the client side, AgentGuard provides lightweight integration for agents implemented in different programming languages and architectures. It requires only minor code modifications (e.g., around 10 lines) without changing the underlying agent execution logic. On the server side, AgentGuard provides three complementary inspection mechanisms to cover both single-tool and cross-tool security risks in agent execution. In addition, it offers a visualized front-end interface for security policy specification and runtime auditing. Currently, AgentGuard is publicly accessible at https://github.com/WhitzardAgent/AgentGuard.

</details>


### [244] [SilentRetrieval: Hijacking Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Semantically-Preserving Adversarial Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28074)
*Jiachen Qian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 SilentRetrieval，一种两阶段数据中毒攻击，通过对抗性流畅文档劫持 RAG 系统，在单毒文档评价下达到 84.6%/81.3% HR@10，并展示跨模型、跨检索器迁移能力及 Wikipedia 规模下 0.016% 投毒比仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 能缓解 LLM 幻觉，但引入了语料库完整性的关键漏洞；现有攻击方法生成的对抗文档往往不流畅，易被检测。

Method: 两阶段攻击：Stage 1 使用 Coordinated Beam Search（多 token 联合优化，含流畅性-相似度目标）保持毒文档可检索且困惑度低；Stage 2 使用 Context-Adaptive Trigger Generation（基于冻结 LLM 的轻量触发融合）将操纵触发器嵌入文档内容。

Result: NQ/MS MARCO 上 HR@10 达 84.6%/81.3%，ASR-LLM 达 57.5%/54.8%；跨 4 种 LLM 迁移有效；对 ColBERT 等未见检索器迁移 HR@10 平均 64.7%；Wikipedia 规模下 0.016% 投毒比仍保持 74.2% HR@10；防御可降低成功率但带来延迟权衡。

Conclusion: SilentRetrieval 揭示了 RAG 系统在语料库完整性方面的严重安全威胁，即使在极低投毒比下也能高效劫持检索结果，亟需更有效的联合防御机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates LLM hallucinations but introduces a critical vulnerability: corpus integrity. We present SilentRetrieval, a two-stage data poisoning attack that hijacks RAG systems through adversarially crafted yet fluent documents. Stage 1 uses Coordinated Beam Search, a multi-token joint optimization method with a fluency-similarity objective, to keep a poisoned host document retrievable while constraining perplexity. Stage 2 uses Context-Adaptive Trigger Generation, a lightweight trigger-fusion step driven by a frozen LLM, to integrate manipulation triggers into document content. Under a one-poisoned-document-per-query evaluation with synthetic target answers, SilentRetrieval achieves 84.6%/81.3% HR@10 and 57.5%/54.8% ASR-LLM on Natural Questions and MS MARCO, while maintaining near-benign perplexity. Cross-model evaluation across four target LLMs shows nontrivial effectiveness under a fixed trigger generator, and transfer tests against unseen retrievers, including ColBERT and commercial embedding models, yield 64.7% average HR@10 under the same injected-corpus protocol. In a sampled Wikipedia-scale evaluation, SilentRetrieval retains 74.2% HR@10 at a 0.016% poisoning ratio. Combined retrieval-side and generation-side defenses reduce attack success substantially but incur a latency trade-off. Human evaluation shows substantially lower flag rates than disfluent baselines, while remaining numerically more suspicious than benign content at the current sample size.

</details>


### [245] [Mind the Gap: Mixtures of Gaussians in Approximate Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28078)
*Huikang Liu,Aras Selvi,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一类混合噪声机制（Mixture Mechanisms），通过混合多个同方差但不同均值和权重的高斯分布，实现对标量实值查询函数的(ε,δ)-差分隐私保护，在中低隐私预算下显著降低噪声幅度和方差，逼近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解析高斯机制在中低隐私预算下存在较大的最优性差距，需要设计更优的加噪机制来降低噪声幅度和方差，提升数据效用。

Method: 构造混合机制：将零均值高斯（解析高斯机制所用）与多个均值依赖敏感度的高斯分布进行凸组合，推导了满足(ε,δ)-DP的严格方差条件，并设计了高效计算方差参数的算法。

Result: 相比解析高斯机制，混合机制在l1损失（期望噪声幅度）和l2损失（零均值分布方差）上均显著降低；在低隐私预算下几乎完全消除了解析高斯机制的最优性差距。

Conclusion: 混合机制在中低隐私预算下是一种逼近最优的差分隐私加噪方案，能有效弥补现有高斯机制的性能不足。

Abstract: We design a class of additive noise mechanisms that satisfy \((\varepsilon, δ)\)-differential privacy (DP) for scalar, real-valued query functions with known sensitivities, with a particular focus on moderate and low-privacy regimes. These mechanisms, which we call \textit{mixture mechanisms}, are constructed by mixing multiple Gaussian distributions that share the same variance but differ in their means and mixture weights. The resulting distributions can be interpreted as convex combinations of a zero-mean Gaussian (as used in the analytic Gaussian mechanism) and additional Gaussians whose means depend on the sensitivity of the query function. We derive tight conditions on the variances required for \((\varepsilon, δ)\)-DP and provide efficient algorithms to compute them. Compared to the analytic Gaussian mechanism, our mechanisms yield substantially lower expected noise amplitudes (\(l_1\)-loss) and variances (\(l_2\)-loss for zero-mean distributions). In the low-privacy regime that motivates our design, our mechanisms approach optimality, mitigating nearly all of the optimality gap of the analytic Gaussian mechanism.

</details>


### [246] [SNARE: Adaptive Scenario Synthesis for Eliciting Overeager Behavior in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28122)
*Yubin Qu,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Yanjun Zhang,Yuekang Li,Ying Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SNARE流水线，通过非对抗性场景组合与无评判器检测，系统评估编码智能体在执行良性任务时的过度行为（越权操作），发现19.5%的良性运行触发此类行为，且智能体框架比基座模型贡献更多变异性（56% vs 21%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法有效检测编码智能体在执行良性任务时产生的越权行为（如凭据泄露、文件删除），任务完成基准只关注任务是否成功，越狱基准针对对抗性提示，而仅有的过激行为基准使用固定提示集，导致不同智能体-模型组合的测量不充分。

Method: 提出SNARE流水线：1) 从可复用的作用域与陷阱片段组合生成非对抗场景；2) 使用无评判器的预言机，通过陷阱模式匹配以及未授权的文件增删操作来标记过激行为；3) 采用Thompson采样将运行预算动态分配到最易触发过激行为的场景。在24种过激行为原型上实例化得到OverEager基准，覆盖4种编码智能体×5种基座模型。

Result: 在10,000次良性运行中，19.51%触发了过激行为，智能体-模型对之间的触发率差异达11.9倍。框架贡献了56%的变异，模型仅贡献21%，表明单一框架或单一模型的评估会低估约五分之一的矩阵内差异。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的过激行为是普遍且严重的问题，评估时必须同时覆盖多种智能体框架和基座模型，否则会显著低估风险。SNARE流水线提供了一种可扩展、无评判器偏见的自动化评估方法。

Abstract: A coding agent executes a benign task as a sequence of shell, file, and network actions, any of which can quietly exceed the authorized scope while the task still completes. We call this overeager behavior: the prompt is not adversarial and the run succeeds, yet an out-of-scope step can leak credentials or delete files. Existing benchmarks miss it: task-completion suites credit any finished run, jailbreak suites probe adversarial prompts, and the one prior overeager benchmark applies a single fixed prompt set to every agent-model pair, leaving its easiest and most resistant pairs under-measured. We present SNARE (Synthesizing Non-adversarial scenarios for Adaptive Reward-guided Elicitation), a pipeline that composes benign scenarios from reusable scope and trap fragments, scores each run with a judge-free oracle flagging trap-pattern matches and unsolicited file additions or deletions, and uses Thompson sampling to steer each pair's run budget toward the scenarios that most often trigger it. Instantiating it over 24 overeager archetypes yields OverEager, which we run across a 4x5 matrix of four coding agents and five base models. Across 10,000 benign runs, 19.51% trigger overeager behavior, with per-pair rates spanning 11.9x. This variation is driven by the agent framework, not the model: the framework accounts for 56% of it against the model's 21%, so any single-framework or single-model evaluation undercounts the matrix by about a fifth.

</details>


### [247] [Out of Sight, Not Out of Mind: Unveiling Latent Attack in Latent-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28214)
*Chenxi Wang,Ruiyang Huang,Jiayan Sun,Lei Wei,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于潜在表示的多智能体系统中，隐蔽表示可以携带攻击相关信息，并在干净执行中保持有效性。作者提出了潜在攻击框架，通过潜在干预而非复用对抗文本来重新激活攻击效应。实验表明，这种仅潜在攻击能显著降低任务性能，尤其是在智能体间KV缓存传递中。研究指出潜在协作并未消除攻击风险，而是将其转移到了更不易观察的执行状态中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨基于潜在表示的多智能体系统中，潜在状态是否能携带攻击相关信息，并在干净执行中保持有效，从而揭示潜在空间协作带来的新安全风险。

Method: 提出潜在攻击框架（latent attack framework），通过潜在干预（latent interventions）重新激活攻击诱导效应，无需复用对抗文本。

Result: 仅潜在攻击能显著降低干净执行中的任务性能，尤其是针对智能体间KV缓存传递的攻击效果最为明显；且这种性能下降不能归因于任意扰动或无效生成。

Conclusion: 基于潜在表示的协作并未消除攻击风险，而是将部分风险转移至更不易观察的执行状态，需要超越可见文本检查的安全防护措施。

Abstract: Latent-based multi-agent systems replace parts of explicit inter-agent communication with hidden representations, offering a new direction for efficient and flexible agent collaboration. However, moving coordination into latent space may also move attacks beyond the reach of visible-text inspection. In this paper, we study whether latent states can carry attack-associated information that remains effective during clean executions. To examine this question, we introduce a latent attack framework that reactivates attack-induced effects through latent interventions without reusing adversarial text. Extensive experiments show that the resulting latent-only attacks can substantially degrade task performance in clean executions, especially when applied to inter-agent KV-cache handoffs rather than local hidden states. Further control analyses indicate that this degradation cannot be reduced to arbitrary perturbations or invalid generation. Overall, our findings suggest that latent-based collaboration does not remove attack risk. It shifts part of the risk into less observable execution states, calling for safeguards beyond visible-text inspection.

</details>


### [248] [Do you dare to try Test-Driven Forensics? Increasing Trust in Desktop Forensics with ADARE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28476)
*Michael Külper,Martin Lambertz,Mariia Rybalka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出测试驱动取证（Test-Driven Forensics）方法，将取证预期编码为可执行测试，通过状态转换测试验证用户操作后的系统状态，并实现跨版本的回归检测。实现框架ADARE通过虚拟机控制实验和视觉引导GUI自动化模拟用户活动，在五个案例研究中验证了有效性，其中对Autopsy的25版本回归研究揭示了大量未文档化的输出变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统、应用和分析工具的快速演化导致工件行为和工具输出发生漂移， silently degrading重复性和长期取证解释的置信度。现有方法缺乏对跨版本回归的系统性检测机制。

Method: 提出测试驱动取证方法：1) 将预期工件和预期工具输出编码为可执行测试；2) 引入状态转换测试，验证每次用户操作后的系统预期状态，而非仅对最终磁盘镜像进行事后检查；3) 实现ADARE开源框架，在虚拟机中运行受控实验，通过计算机视觉引导的GUI自动化模拟用户活动；4) 配套Web平台用于共享实验、环境和结果，支持独立重跑和同行验证。

Result: 五个案例研究覆盖工件研究和工具验证。对Autopsy的25版本回归研究揭示了大量未文档化的导出报告输出变化，证明可执行测试能够可测量、可复现地检测漂移。

Conclusion: 测试驱动取证使漂移变得可测量且可大规模复现，提升了数字取证中解释的重复性和置信度。

Abstract: Digital forensic relies on validated tools and established procedures, yet the underlying operating systems, applications, and analysis tools evolve rapidly. This evolution can cause artifact behavior and tool outputs to drift, silently degrading repeatability and confidence in long-lived forensic interpretations. We present test-driven forensics, a practical approach that treats forensic expectations as executable specifications: expected artifacts and expected tool outputs are encoded as tests that can be rerun across versions to detect regressions. Crucially, our approach also enables State Transition Testing, validating the system's expected state after each user action rather than only performing post-mortem checks on a final disk image; this supports causal attribution and makes transient behavior testable. We implement the methodology in ADARE, an open-source framework that runs controlled experiments in virtual machines and simulates realistic user activity via computer-vision-guided GUI automation. ADARE includes a companion web platform for sharing experiments, environments, and results to facilitate independent reruns and peer verification. We evaluate ADARE in five case studies spanning artifact research and tool validation. In particular, a 25-version regression study of Autopsy reveals substantial, largely undocumented changes in exported report outputs, demonstrating how executable tests make drift measurable and reproducible at scale.

</details>


### [249] [Technical Report: Exploring the Emerging Threats of the Agent Skill Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28588)
*Luca Beurer-Kellner,Aleksei Kudrinskii,Marco Milanta,Kristian Bonde Nielsen,Hemang Sarkar,Liran Tal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对3,984个AI agent技能进行分析，发现76个恶意载荷（凭证窃取、后门、数据泄露），13.4%的技能存在至少一个严重安全问题，截至发表日仍有至少8个确认的恶意技能在clawhub.ai上公开可用。本文记录了分析方法、威胁分类和攻击模式，强调技能市场快速增长下自动化安全分析的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent技能市场快速增长，技能可访问敏感凭证和系统，但缺乏系统性的安全分析，恶意技能可能造成严重安全威胁。

Method: 对来自主流市场的3,984个AI agent技能进行自动化安全分析，检测恶意载荷，并对发现的威胁进行分类和模式分析。

Result: 发现了76个确认的恶意载荷（包括凭证窃取、后门安装、数据外泄），13.4%的技能存在至少一个严重安全问题，至少8个确认的恶意技能在发表时仍可公开访问。

Conclusion: AI agent技能市场存在严重安全漏洞，自动化安全分析对于保护用户免受恶意技能侵害已变得不可或缺。

Abstract: We analyzed 3,984 AI agent skills from major marketplaces and found 76 confirmed malicious payloads, including credential theft, backdoor installation, and data exfiltration. 13.4% of all skills contain at least one critical-level security issue and at least 8 manually confirmed malicious skills remain publicly available on clawhub.ai as of the date of publication. This report documents our methodology, presents a threat taxonomy based on real-world samples, and details the attack patterns we observed. As skill marketplaces grow rapidly and AI agents gain access to sensitive credentials and systems, automated security analysis is no longer optional.

</details>


### [250] [Position: Retire the "Positive Backdoor" Label -- Secret Alignment Requires Strict and Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28597)
*Jianwei Li,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张AI/ML社区应停止使用"正向后门"这一标签，改用"秘密对齐"来描述触发激活的隐藏行为，并强调此类保护性声明在缺乏严格标准化评估时不应被视为安全可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型和私有AI时代的到来使得语言模型成为私人数字资产，由此产生的未授权访问、模型盗窃和行为滥用等安全问题亟需解决。现有被称为"正向后门"的方法试图应对这些挑战，但缺乏严格的评估标准。

Method: 将现有"正向后门"方案统一为隐蔽触发-行为关联（用于访问控制、所有权归属和安全执行），并对三个代表性应用在六个核心属性（有效性、无害性、持久性、效率、鲁棒性、可靠性）上进行评估。

Result: 评估揭示了触发-行为映射存在显著的脆弱性，特别是在机密性、完整性和可用性（CIA）方面，这些缺陷往往被现有声明所低估。这些结果与行为密度和决策复杂性相关。

Conclusion: 社区应停止使用"正向后门"标签，转而采用"秘密对齐"概念，并通过社区范围的严格评估使秘密对齐声明变得可证明、可验证。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the AI/ML community should stop overclaiming and retire the label "positive backdoor," and instead treat trigger-activated hidden behaviors as Secret Alignment. Crucially, protective claims based on Secret Alignment should be presumed not secure by default unless supported by rigorous, standardized evaluation. The Private AI era, enabled by open-weight LLMs and accessible training/inference stacks, turns language models into privately owned digital assets, creating security concerns around unauthorized access, model theft, and behavioral misuse. Recently, a line of work framed as "positive backdoors" has been proposed to address these challenges. To ground our position in evidence, we unify these proposals as covert trigger-behavior associations for access gating, ownership attribution, and safety enforcement, and evaluate three representative applications across six core properties: effectiveness, harmlessness, persistence, efficiency, robustness, and reliability. Our results reveal substantial brittleness - especially in the confidentiality, integrity, and availability (CIA) - of trigger-behavior mappings often underrepresented by existing claims. We further relate these outcomes to behavior density and decision complexity, offering a behavioral lens for understanding deployment-time risks and motivating community-wide evaluation that makes Secret Alignment claims provable.

</details>


### [251] [Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28632)
*Ziyang You,Huilong He,Xiaoke Yang,Xuxing Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对LLM文本水印的供应链攻击SeedHijack，通过替换伪随机数生成器(PRNG)在无需任何知识的情况下放大水印信号，同时可规避所有现有内容侧检测器，并证明量子随机数生成器(QRNG)可完全防御此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方案的安全性依赖PRNG可信这一假设，但该假设从未被质疑或验证。作者旨在揭示这一安全漏洞，并证明PRNG完整性应成为密码学内容溯源系统的一等安全需求。

Method: SeedHijack攻击在供应链层替换PRNG，通过偏置绿名单选择来放大水印信号，而非扰动输出文本。攻击是盲的（无需水印密钥、检测器或模型logits知识）、保持完整性（放大而非擦除水印信号），且与检测正交（攻击偏置与内容侧检测统计量独立）。

Result: 在三种水印方案（KGW, Unigram, DipMark）和三个开源LLM上，SeedHijack使所有6个最先进的内容侧统计检测器均无法检出（0/6），同时将水印z-score提升至2.42倍。QRNG作为防御手段可完全中和该攻击。

Conclusion: PRNG完整性必须被视为密码学内容溯源系统的一等安全需求，QRNG可作为有效防御措施。

Abstract: Cryptographic watermarking is a leading defense for attributing text generated by large language models (LLMs). Existing schemes, including KGW, Unigram, and DipMark, derive their security guarantees from the assumption that the underlying pseudo-random number generator (PRNG) is trustworthy. This work introduces SeedHijack, the first supply-chain attack on LLM watermarking that is simultaneously (i) blind -- requiring no knowledge of the watermark key, detector, or model logits, (ii) integrity-preserving -- amplifying rather than erasing the watermark signal, and (iii) orthogonal to detection -- the attack-induced bias is statistically independent of all content-side detector statistics, ensuring that amplification and evasion coexist without trade-off. Rather than perturbing generated text, SeedHijack replaces the PRNG at the supply-chain layer, biasing green-list selection without altering output tokens or degrading text quality. Across three watermarking schemes and three open-source LLMs, the attack triggers 0/6 state-of-the-art content-side statistical detectors while inflating the watermark z-score up to 2.42x (system-level defenses such as entropy-source attestation remain orthogonal and complementary). A quantum random number generator (QRNG) countermeasure is shown to fully neutralize the attack while preserving benign watermarking utility. These findings establish PRNG integrity as a first-class security requirement for cryptographic content-provenance systems.

</details>


### [252] [GraphSteal: Structural Knowledge Stealing from Graph RAG via Traversal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28645)
*Jinze Gu,Qinghua Mao,Xi Lin,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示Graph RAG系统存在结构隐私泄露风险，提出一种面向知识图谱重构的结构提取攻击框架，通过深度优先启发式搜索和广度优先扩散搜索，可从黑盒Graph RAG系统中高保真恢复超过90%的隐藏知识图谱，且现有防护措施效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph RAG虽然通过引入知识图谱增强了LLM的检索能力，但结构化知识在提升性能的同时也引入了新的隐私攻击面。现有研究忽视了Graph RAG系统中隐藏知识图谱的结构泄露风险。

Method: 提出结构导向的重构框架：（1）深度优先启发式搜索（Depth-Wise Heuristic Search）：通过递归扩展以实体为中心的证据来提取细粒度节点属性；（2）广度优先扩散搜索（Breadth-Wise Diffusion Search）：通过在关系诱导的邻域间传播来推断图拓扑结构。两者结合实现从局部到全局的知识图谱恢复。

Result: 在通用场景和医疗场景的实验表明，该方法能从代表性Graph RAG系统中恢复超过90%的原始知识图谱，高保真地揭示敏感实体、关系及结构依赖。现有防护措施对该攻击提供的防御效果有限。

Conclusion: Graph RAG管道中的结构隐私保护存在固有困难，强调了在RAG系统中构建结构化知识检索时需重视隐私安全设计。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs by grounding generation in query-relevant external evidence. Beyond unstructured text corpora, Graph RAG integrates knowledge graphs into the retrieval pipeline, enabling LLMs to access entities, relations, and multi-hop dependencies encoded in structured knowledge. However, the same structured knowledge that empowers Graph RAG also creates a new privacy attack surface. We demonstrate that Graph RAG systems can be turned into structural oracles: through adaptive black-box interactions, an adversary can elicit sufficient relational evidence to reconstruct substantial portions of the hidden knowledge graph. We propose a structure-oriented reconstruction framework that recovers targeted graphs from both local and global perspectives. Specifically, Depth-Wise Heuristic Search extracts fine-grained node attributes by recursively expanding entity-centered evidence, while Breadth-Wise Diffusion Search infers graph topology by propagating across relation-induced neighborhoods. Experiments on generic and healthcare scenarios demonstrate that our method can recover over 90\% of the original knowledge graph from representative Graph RAG systems, revealing sensitive entities, relations, and structural dependencies with high fidelity. Existing guradrails provide limited defense against our attack, highlighting the inherent difficulty of safeguarding structural privacy in Graph RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [253] [MaskClaw: Edge-Side Personalized Privacy Arbitration for GUI Agents with Behavior-Driven Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28646)
*Yanqiu Zhao,Dongying Zheng,Kaibo Huang,Yukun Wei,Zhongliang Yang,Linna Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MaskClaw 是一个边缘侧隐私仲裁器，用于 GUI 智能体在截图离设备前，通过本地视觉证据、用户策略记忆和沙箱门控机制，决定是否允许、遮挡或询问，从而保护隐私。论文还提出了 P-GUI-Evo 基准测试，验证了传统方法在隐私保护上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体依赖截图进行跨应用操作，但截图中包含大量敏感信息（如私人消息、医疗记录、支付凭证等）。现有的静态 PII 检测器无法感知任务、接收方、应用状态和用户角色的边界，而云端 VLM 推理在决定保护哪些内容之前可能已经上传原始截图，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: MaskClaw 在边缘侧（用户或组织可控环境）工作，提取本地视觉证据，检索用户和任务特定的策略记忆，并在原始截图离开前做出 Allow（允许）、Mask（遮挡）或 Ask（询问）的决策。同时设计了五种技能演化场景，将用户的纠正、取消和编辑转化为可复用的隐私技能，并由沙箱门控检查。

Result: 在 P-GUI-Evo 基准测试（基于真实 UI 模式、重构 HTML 屏幕和脱敏标签构建）上的实验表明，单纯的模式匹配、云端推理和路由方法在同一协议下容易过度确认、过度遮挡或直接暴露原始截图。

Conclusion: MaskClaw 通过在边缘侧进行隐私仲裁，结合本地证据和用户策略，有效解决了 GUI 智能体场景下的隐私保护问题，优于传统方法。

Abstract: GUI agents rely on screenshots to infer intent and operate across applications, but these screenshots often contain private messages, medical records, payment credentials, and workplace-specific workflows. Privacy decisions in this setting depend on task, recipient, application state, and user role, yet static PII detectors miss these boundaries and cloud-side VLM reasoning can upload the raw screen before deciding what should be protected. We present MaskClaw, an edge-side privacy arbitrator for GUI agents. MaskClaw extracts local visual evidence, retrieves user- and task-specific policy memory, and decides Allow, Mask, or Ask before raw screenshots leave a trusted user- or organization-controlled environment. In five designed skill-evolution scenarios, it turns corrections, cancellations, and edits into reusable privacy skills checked by a sandbox gate. We introduce P-GUI-Evo, a benchmark built from real UI patterns, reconstructed HTML screens, and sanitized labels. Experiments show that pattern matching, cloud reasoning, and routing alone tend to over-confirm, over-mask, or expose raw screenshots under the same protocol. The artifact is available at https://github.com/Theodora-Y/MaskClaw.

</details>


### [254] [Code as a Weapon: A Consensus-Labeled Prompt Bank for Measuring Coding-Model Compliance with Malicious-Code Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28734)
*Richard J. Young,Gregory D. Moody*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现代码模型生成可执行恶意代码（如键盘记录器、勒索软件）的严重性远超通用语言模型的安全回复，因此代码模型应具备更高的拒绝标准。然而现有基准碎片化，混淆了可执行代码请求与安全知识请求。为此，作者构建了一个经五名标注者共识标注的大规模提示库（6,675条提示，Fleiss' kappa=0.767），区分了4,748条可执行恶意代码请求（CODE）和1,923条有害安全知识请求（KNOWLEDGE），为代码模型安全对齐提供了可靠评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型一次违规就可能生成可直接运行的恶意武器（如勒索软件、漏洞利用），其危害严重性远超通用语言模型。然而现有拒绝基准碎片化，混淆了可执行代码请求与安全知识请求，且缺乏跨语料库的标准度量，无法评估代码模型是否真正达到了更高的拒绝标准。

Method: 收集并整合了8个现有语料库（ASTRA、CySecBench等共6,675条提示），采用5名标注者共识标注协议（共33,375次标注调用），区分"可执行恶意代码请求（CODE）"和"有害安全知识请求（KNOWLEDGE）"，计算Fleiss' kappa评估一致性，通过多数投票确定最终标签。

Result: 五名标注者达到Fleiss' kappa=0.767（95% CI [0.755, 0.777]），"高度一致"；95.0%的提示获得至少4名标注者一致意见，76.9%获得全票一致；最终发布包含4,748条CODE提示和1,923条KNOWLEDGE提示的标注库。

Conclusion: 该提示库是首个经过信度量化的代码模型安全对齐评估工具，为检验代码模型是否达到可执行输出所需的更严格拒绝标准提供了验证基础。

Abstract: A general-purpose language model that answers a harmful question returns text; a coding model that complies with a malicious request can return a working weapon -- a keylogger, a ransomware stub, an exploit that runs as written. This asymmetry in the severity of a single act of compliance implies coding-specialized models should clear a higher refusal bar than general-purpose chat models, not a lower one, yet the field cannot presently tell whether they do. Refusal benchmarks for malicious code are fragmented: they mix requests for executable software (ready-to-run weapons) with requests for harmful security knowledge (information a human must still operationalise) and report refusal rates over non-comparable corpora, so no single statistic measures the property that actually matters. This paper introduces an expanded consensus-labeled prompt bank that distinguishes between these two request types and provides a construct-stable substrate for cross-corpus coding-model compliance measurement. Eight corpora (ASTRA, CySecBench, AdvBench/harmful_behaviors, JailbreakBench, MalwareBench, RedCode, RMCBench, Scam2Prompt) are consolidated and classified under a five-judge consensus protocol (6,675 prompts x 5 judges = 33,375 calls). The panel reaches Fleiss' kappa = 0.767 [95% CI 0.755, 0.777] ("substantial"); 95.0% of prompts draw at least four agreeing judges, 76.9% are unanimous, and the panel reproduces the earlier four-corpus release at Cohen's kappa = 0.952 on the 3,133 shared prompts. The released bank comprises 4,748 consensus-CODE prompts (executable malicious code requests) and 1,923 consensus-KNOWLEDGE prompts (harmful security knowledge requests). The bank is the validated instrument the field has lacked: a reliability-quantified basis for testing whether coding models meet the stricter refusal standard their executable output demands.

</details>
